MAT 1:1 1 The book of the generation of Jesus Christ, the son of David, the son of Abraham.
MAT 1:2 2 Abraham begat Isaac; and Isaac begat Jacob; and Jacob begat Judah and his brethren;
MAT 1:3 3 and Judah begat Perez and Zerah of Tamar; and Perez begat Hezron; and Hezron begat Ram;
MAT 1:4 4 and Ram begat Amminadab; and Amminadab begat Nahshon; and Nahshon begat Salmon;
MAT 1:5 5 and Salmon begat Boaz of Rahab; and Boaz begat Obed of Ruth; and Obed begat Jesse;
MAT 1:6 6 and Jesse begat David the king.
MAT 1:7 And David begat Solomon of her that had been the wife of Uriah;
MAT 1:8 7 and Solomon begat Rehoboam; and Rehoboam begat Abijah; and Abijah begat Asa;
MAT 1:9 8 and Asa begat Jehoshaphat; and Jehoshaphat begat Joram; and Joram begat Uzziah;
MAT 1:10 9 and Uzziah begat Jotham; and Jotham begat Ahaz; and Ahaz begat Hezekiah;
MAT 1:11 10 and Hezekiah begat Manasseh; and Manasseh begat Amon; and Amon begat Josiah;
MAT 1:12 11 and Josiah begat Jechoniah and his brethren, at the time of the carrying away to Babylon.
MAT 1:13 12 And after the carrying away to Babylon, Jechoniah begat Shealtiel; and Shealtiel begat Zerubbabel;
MAT 1:14 13 and Zerubbabel begat Abiud; and Abiud begat Eliakim; and Eliakim begat Azor;
MAT 1:15 14 and Azor begat Sadoc; and Sadoc begat Achim; and Achim begat Eliud;
MAT 1:16 15 and Eliud begat Eleazar; and Eleazar begat Matthan; and Matthan begat Jacob;
MAT 1:17 16 and Jacob begat Joseph the husband of Mary, of whom was born Jesus, who is called Christ.
MAT 1:18 17 So all the generations from Abraham unto David are fourteen generations; and from David unto the carrying away to Babylon fourteen generations; and from the carrying away to Babylon unto the Christ fourteen generations.
MAT 1:19 18 Now the birth of Jesus Christ was on this wise: When his mother Mary had been betrothed to Joseph, before they came together she was found with child of the Holy Spirit.
MAT 1:20 19 And Joseph her husband, being a righteous man, and not willing to make her a public example, was minded to put her away privily.
MAT 1:21 20 But when he thought on these things, behold, an angel of the Lord appeared unto him in a dream, saying, Joseph, thou son of David, fear not to take unto thee Mary thy wife: for that which is conceived in her is of the Holy Spirit.
MAT 1:22 21 And she shall bring forth a son; and thou shalt call his name JESUS; for it is he that shall save his people from their sins.
MAT 1:23 22 Now all this is come to pass, that it might be fulfilled which was spoken by the Lord through the prophet, saying,
MAT 1:24 23 Behold, the virgin shall be with child, and shall bring forth a son,
MAT 1:25 And they shall call his name Immanuel;
MAT 1:26 which is, being interpreted, God with us.
MAT 1:27 24 And Joseph arose from his sleep, and did as the angel of the Lord commanded him, and took unto him his wife;
MAT 1:28 25 and knew her not till she had brought forth a son: and he called his name JESUS.
MAT 2:1 1 Now when Jesus was born in Bethlehem of Judæa in the days of Herod the king, behold, Wise-men from the east came to Jerusalem, saying,
MAT 2:2 2 Where is he that is born King of the Jews? for we saw his star in the east, and are come to worship him.
MAT 2:3 3 And when Herod the king heard it, he was troubled, and all Jerusalem with him.
MAT 2:4 4 And gathering together all the chief priests and scribes of the people, he inquired of them where the Christ should be born.
MAT 2:5 5 And they said unto him, In Bethlehem of Judæa: for thus it is written through the prophet,
MAT 2:6 6 And thou Bethlehem, land of Judah,
MAT 2:7 Art in no wise least among the princes of Judah:
MAT 2:8 For out of thee shall come forth a governor,
MAT 2:9 Who shall be shepherd of my people Israel.
MAT 2:10 7 Then Herod privily called the Wise-men, and learned of them exactly what time the star appeared.
MAT 2:11 8 And he sent them to Bethlehem, and said, Go and search out exactly concerning the young child; and when ye have found him , bring me word, that I also may come and worship him.
MAT 2:12 9 And they, having heard the king, went their way; and lo, the star, which they saw in the east, went before them, till it came and stood over where the young child was.
MAT 2:13 10 And when they saw the star, they rejoiced with exceeding great joy.
MAT 2:14 11 And they came into the house and saw the young child with Mary his mother; and they fell down and worshipped him; and opening their treasures they offered unto him gifts, gold and frankincense and myrrh.
MAT 2:15 12 And being warned of God in a dream that they should not return to Herod, they departed into their own country another way.
MAT 2:16 13 Now when they were departed, behold, an angel of the Lord appeareth to Joseph in a dream, saying, Arise and take the young child and his mother, and flee into Egypt, and be thou there until I tell thee: for Herod will seek the young child to destroy him.
MAT 2:17 14 And he arose and took the young child and his mother by night, and departed into Egypt;
MAT 2:18 15 and was there until the death of Herod: that it might be fulfilled which was spoken by the Lord through the prophet, saying, Out of Egypt did I call my son.
MAT 2:19 16 Then Herod, when he saw that he was mocked of the Wise-men, was exceeding wroth, and sent forth, and slew all the male children that were in Bethlehem, and in all the borders thereof, from two years old and under, according to the time which he had exactly learned of the Wise-men.
MAT 2:20 17 Then was fulfilled that which was spoken through Jeremiah the prophet, saying,
MAT 2:21 18 A voice was heard in Ramah,
MAT 2:22 Weeping and great mourning,
MAT 2:23 Rachel weeping for her children;
MAT 2:24 And she would not be comforted, because they are not.
MAT 2:25 19 But when Herod was dead, behold, an angel of the Lord appeareth in a dream to Joseph in Egypt, saying,
MAT 2:26 20 Arise and take the young child and his mother, and go into the land of Israel: for they are dead that sought the young child’s life.
MAT 2:27 21 And he arose and took the young child and his mother, and came into the land of Israel.
MAT 2:28 22 But when he heard that Archelaus was reigning over Judæa in the room of his father Herod, he was afraid to go thither; and being warned of God in a dream, he withdrew into the parts of Galilee,
MAT 2:29 23 and came and dwelt in a city called Nazareth; that it might be fulfilled which was spoken through the prophets, that he should be called a Nazarene.
MAT 3:1 1 And in those days cometh John the Baptist, preaching in the wilderness of Judæa, saying,
MAT 3:2 2 Repent ye; for the kingdom of heaven is at hand.
MAT 3:3 3 For this is he that was spoken of through Isaiah the prophet, saying,
MAT 3:4 The voice of one crying in the wilderness,
MAT 3:5 Make ye ready the way of the Lord,
MAT 3:6 Make his paths straight.
MAT 3:7 4 Now John himself had his raiment of camel’s hair, and a leathern girdle about his loins; and his food was locusts and wild honey.
MAT 3:8 5 Then went out unto him Jerusalem, and all Judæa, and all the region round about the Jordan;
MAT 3:9 6 and they were baptized of him in the river Jordan, confessing their sins.
MAT 3:10 7 But when he saw many of the Pharisees and Sadducees coming to his baptism, he said unto them, Ye offspring of vipers, who warned you to flee from the wrath to come?
MAT 3:11 8 Bring forth therefore fruit worthy of repentance:
MAT 3:12 9 and think not to say within yourselves, We have Abraham to our father: for I say unto you, that God is able of these stones to raise up children unto Abraham.
MAT 3:13 10 And even now the axe lieth at the root of the trees: every tree therefore that bringeth not forth good fruit is hewn down, and cast into the fire.
MAT 3:14 11 I indeed baptize you in water unto repentance: but he that cometh after me is mightier than I, whose shoes I am not worthy to bear: he shall baptize you in the Holy Spirit and in fire:
MAT 3:15 12 whose fan is in his hand, and he will thoroughly cleanse his threshing-floor; and he will gather his wheat into the garner, but the chaff he will burn up with unquenchable fire.
MAT 3:16 13 Then cometh Jesus from Galilee to the Jordan unto John, to be baptized of him.
MAT 3:17 14 But John would have hindered him, saying, I have need to be baptized of thee, and comest thou to me?
MAT 3:18 15 But Jesus answering said unto him, Suffer it now: for thus it becometh us to fulfil all righteousness. Then he suffereth him.
MAT 3:19 16 And Jesus, when he was baptized, went up straightway from the water: and lo, the heavens were opened unto him, and he saw the Spirit of God descending as a dove, and coming upon him;
MAT 3:20 17 and lo, a voice out of the heavens, saying, This is my beloved Son, in whom I am well pleased.
MAT 4:1 1 Then was Jesus led up of the Spirit into the wilderness to be tempted of the devil.
MAT 4:2 2 And when he had fasted forty days and forty nights, he afterward hungered.
MAT 4:3 3 And the tempter came and said unto him, If thou art the Son of God, command that these stones become bread.
MAT 4:4 4 But he answered and said, It is written, Man shall not live by bread alone, but by every word that proceedeth out of the mouth of God.
MAT 4:5 5 Then the devil taketh him into the holy city; and he set him on the pinnacle of the temple,
MAT 4:6 6 and saith unto him, If thou art the Son of God, cast thyself down: for it is written,
MAT 4:7 He shall give his angels charge concerning thee:
MAT 4:8 and,
MAT 4:9 On their hands they shall bear thee up,
MAT 4:10 Lest haply thou dash thy foot against a stone.
MAT 4:11 7 Jesus said unto him, Again it is written, Thou shalt not make trial of the Lord thy God.
MAT 4:12 8 Again, the devil taketh him unto an exceeding high mountain, and showeth him all the kingdoms of the world, and the glory of them;
MAT 4:13 9 and he said unto him, All these things will I give thee, if thou wilt fall down and worship me.
MAT 4:14 10 Then saith Jesus unto him, Get thee hence, Satan: for it is written, Thou shalt worship the Lord thy God, and him only shalt thou serve.
MAT 4:15 11 Then the devil leaveth him; and behold, angels came and ministered unto him.
MAT 4:16 12 Now when he heard that John was delivered up, he withdrew into Galilee;
MAT 4:17 13 and leaving Nazareth, he came and dwelt in Capernaum, which is by the sea, in the borders of Zebulun and Naphtali:
MAT 4:18 14 that it might be fulfilled which was spoken through Isaiah the prophet, saying,
MAT 4:19 15 The land of Zebulun and the land of Naphtali,
MAT 4:20 Toward the sea, beyond the Jordan,
MAT 4:21 Galilee of the Gentiles,
MAT 4:22 16 The people that sat in darkness
MAT 4:23 Saw a great light,
MAT 4:24 And to them that sat in the region and shadow of death,
MAT 4:25 To them did light spring up.
MAT 4:26 17 From that time began Jesus to preach, and to say, Repent ye; for the kingdom of heaven is at hand.
MAT 4:27 18 And walking by the sea of Galilee, he saw two brethren, Simon who is called Peter, and Andrew his brother, casting a net into the sea; for they were fishers.
MAT 4:28 19 And he saith unto them, Come ye after me, and I will make you fishers of men.
MAT 4:29 20 And they straightway left the nets, and followed him.
MAT 4:30 21 And going on from thence he saw two other brethren, James the son of Zebedee, and John his brother, in the boat with Zebedee their father, mending their nets; and he called them.
MAT 4:31 22 And they straightway left the boat and their father, and followed him.
MAT 4:32 23 And Jesus went about in all Galilee, teaching in their synagogues, and preaching the gospel of the kingdom, and healing all manner of disease and all manner of sickness among the people.
MAT 4:33 24 And the report of him went forth into all Syria: and they brought unto him all that were sick, holden with divers diseases and torments, possessed with demons, and epileptic, and palsied; and he healed them.
MAT 4:34 25 And there followed him great multitudes from Galilee and Decapolis and Jerusalem and Judæa and from beyond the Jordan.
MAT 5:1 1 And seeing the multitudes, he went up into the mountain: and when he had sat down, his disciples came unto him:
MAT 5:2 2 and he opened his mouth and taught them, saying,
MAT 5:3 3 Blessed are the poor in spirit: for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.
MAT 5:4 4 Blessed are they that mourn: for they shall be comforted.
MAT 5:5 5 Blessed are the meek: for they shall inherit the earth.
MAT 5:6 6 Blessed are they that hunger and thirst after righteousness: for they shall be filled.
MAT 5:7 7 Blessed are the merciful: for they shall obtain mercy.
MAT 5:8 8 Blessed are the pure in heart: for they shall see God.
MAT 5:9 9 Blessed are the peacemakers: for they shall be called sons of God.
MAT 5:10 10 Blessed are they that have been persecuted for righteousness’ sake: for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.
MAT 5:11 11 Blessed are ye when men shall reproach you, and persecute you, and say all manner of evil against you falsely, for my sake.
MAT 5:12 12 Rejoice, and be exceeding glad: for great is your reward in heaven: for so persecuted they the prophets that were before you.
MAT 5:13 13 Ye are the salt of the earth: but if the salt have lost its savor, wherewith shall it be salted? it is thenceforth good for nothing, but to be cast out and trodden under foot of men.
MAT 5:14 14 Ye are the light of the world. A city set on a hill cannot be hid.
MAT 5:15 15 Neither do men light a lamp, and put it under the bushel, but on the stand; and it shineth unto all that are in the house.
MAT 5:16 16 Even so let your light shine before men; that they may see your good works, and glorify your Father who is in heaven.
MAT 5:17 17 Think not that I came to destroy the law or the prophets: I came not to destroy, but to fulfil.
MAT 5:18 18 For verily I say unto you, Till heaven and earth pass away, one jot or one tittle shall in no wise pass away from the law, till all things be accomplished.
MAT 5:19 19 Whosoever therefore shall break one of these least commandments, and shall teach men so, shall be called least in the kingdom of heaven: but whosoever shall do and teach them, he shall be called great in the kingdom of heaven.
MAT 5:20 20 For I say unto you, that except your righteousness shall exceed the righteousness of the scribes and Pharisees, ye shall in no wise enter into the kingdom of heaven.
MAT 5:21 21 Ye have heard that it was said to them of old time, Thou shalt not kill; and whosoever shall kill shall be in danger of the judgment:
MAT 5:22 22 but I say unto you, that every one who is angry with his brother shall be in danger of the judgment; and whosoever shall say to his brother, Raca, shall be in danger of the council; and whosoever shall say, Thou fool, shall be in danger of the hell of fire.
MAT 5:23 23 If therefore thou art offering thy gift at the altar, and there rememberest that thy brother hath aught against thee,
MAT 5:24 24 leave there thy gift before the altar, and go thy way, first be reconciled to thy brother, and then come and offer thy gift.
MAT 5:25 25 Agree with thine adversary quickly, while thou art with him in the way; lest haply the adversary deliver thee to the judge, and the judge deliver thee to the officer, and thou be cast into prison.
MAT 5:26 26 Verily I say unto thee, Thou shalt by no means come out thence, till thou have paid the last farthing.
MAT 5:27 27 Ye have heard that it was said, Thou shalt not commit adultery:
MAT 5:28 28 but I say unto you, that every one that looketh on a woman to lust after her hath committed adultery with her already in his heart.
MAT 5:29 29 And if thy right eye causeth thee to stumble, pluck it out, and cast it from thee: for it is profitable for thee that one of thy members should perish, and not thy whole body be cast into hell.
MAT 5:30 30 And if thy right hand causeth thee to stumble, cut it off, and cast it from thee: for it is profitable for thee that one of thy members should perish, and not thy whole body go into hell.
MAT 5:31 31 It was said also, Whosoever shall put away his wife, let him give her a writing of divorcement:
MAT 5:32 32 but I say unto you, that every one that putteth away his wife, saving for the cause of fornication, maketh her an adulteress: and whosoever shall marry her when she is put away committeth adultery.
MAT 5:33 33 Again, ye have heard that it was said to them of old time, Thou shalt not forswear thyself, but shalt perform unto the Lord thine oaths:
MAT 5:34 34 but I say unto you, Swear not at all; neither by the heaven, for it is the throne of God;
MAT 5:35 35 nor by the earth, for it is the footstool of his feet; nor by Jerusalem, for it is the city of the great King.
MAT 5:36 36 Neither shalt thou swear by thy head, for thou canst not make one hair white or black.
MAT 5:37 37 But let your speech be, Yea, yea; Nay, nay: and whatsoever is more than these is of the evil one .
MAT 5:38 38 Ye have heard that it was said, An eye for an eye, and a tooth for a tooth:
MAT 5:39 39 but I say unto you, Resist not him that is evil: but whosoever smiteth thee on thy right cheek, turn to him the other also.
MAT 5:40 40 And if any man would go to law with thee, and take away thy coat, let him have thy cloak also.
MAT 5:41 41 And whosoever shall compel thee to go one mile, go with him two.
MAT 5:42 42 Give to him that asketh thee, and from him that would borrow of thee turn not thou away.
MAT 5:43 43 Ye have heard that it was said, Thou shalt love thy neighbor, and hate thine enemy:
MAT 5:44 44 but I say unto you, Love your enemies, and pray for them that persecute you;
MAT 5:45 45 that ye may be sons of your Father who is in heaven: for he maketh his sun to rise on the evil and the good, and sendeth rain on the just and the unjust.
MAT 5:46 46 For if ye love them that love you, what reward have ye? do not even the publicans the same?
MAT 5:47 47 And if ye salute your brethren only, what do ye more than others ? do not even the Gentiles the same?
MAT 5:48 48 Ye therefore shall be perfect, as your heavenly Father is perfect.
MAT 6:1 1 Take heed that ye do not your righteousness before men, to be seen of them: else ye have no reward with your Father who is in heaven.
MAT 6:2 2 When therefore thou doest alms, sound not a trumpet before thee, as the hypocrites do in the synagogues and in the streets, that they may have glory of men. Verily I say unto you, They have received their reward.
MAT 6:3 3 But when thou doest alms, let not thy left hand know what thy right hand doeth:
MAT 6:4 4 that thine alms may be in secret: and thy Father who seeth in secret shall recompense thee.
MAT 6:5 5 And when ye pray, ye shall not be as the hypocrites: for they love to stand and pray in the synagogues and in the corners of the streets, that they may be seen of men. Verily I say unto you, They have received their reward.
MAT 6:6 6 But thou, when thou prayest, enter into thine inner chamber, and having shut thy door, pray to thy Father who is in secret, and thy Father who seeth in secret shall recompense thee.
MAT 6:7 7 And in praying use not vain repetitions, as the Gentiles do: for they think that they shall be heard for their much speaking.
MAT 6:8 8 Be not therefore like unto them: for your Father knoweth what things ye have need of, before ye ask him.
MAT 6:9 9 After this manner therefore pray ye: Our Father who art in heaven, Hallowed be thy name.
MAT 6:10 10 Thy kingdom come. Thy will be done, as in heaven, so on earth.
MAT 6:11 11 Give us this day our daily bread.
MAT 6:12 12 And forgive us our debts, as we also have forgiven our debtors.
MAT 6:13 13 And bring us not into temptation, but deliver us from the evil one .
MAT 6:14 14 For if ye forgive men their trespasses, your heavenly Father will also forgive you.
MAT 6:15 15 But if ye forgive not men their trespasses, neither will your Father forgive your trespasses.
MAT 6:16 16 Moreover when ye fast, be not, as the hypocrites, of a sad countenance: for they disfigure their faces, that they may be seen of men to fast. Verily I say unto you, They have received their reward.
MAT 6:17 17 But thou, when thou fastest, anoint thy head, and wash thy face;
MAT 6:18 18 that thou be not seen of men to fast, but of thy Father who is in secret: and thy Father, who seeth in secret, shall recompense thee.
MAT 6:19 19 Lay not up for yourselves treasures upon the earth, where moth and rust consume, and where thieves break through and steal:
MAT 6:20 20 but lay up for yourselves treasures in heaven, where neither moth nor rust doth consume, and where thieves do not break through nor steal:
MAT 6:21 21 for where thy treasure is, there will thy heart be also.
MAT 6:22 22 The lamp of the body is the eye: if therefore thine eye be single, thy whole body shall be full of light.
MAT 6:23 23 But if thine eye be evil, thy whole body shall be full of darkness. If therefore the light that is in thee be darkness, how great is the darkness!
MAT 6:24 24 No man can serve two masters: for either he will hate the one, and love the other; or else he will hold to one, and despise the other. Ye cannot serve God and mammon.
MAT 6:25 25 Therefore I say unto you, Be not anxious for your life, what ye shall eat, or what ye shall drink; nor yet for your body, what ye shall put on. Is not the life more than the food, and the body than the raiment?
MAT 6:26 26 Behold the birds of the heaven, that they sow not, neither do they reap, nor gather into barns; and your heavenly Father feedeth them. Are not ye of much more value than they?
MAT 6:27 27 And which of you by being anxious can add one cubit unto the measure of his life?
MAT 6:28 28 And why are ye anxious concerning raiment? Consider the lilies of the field, how they grow; they toil not, neither do they spin:
MAT 6:29 29 yet I say unto you, that even Solomon in all his glory was not arrayed like one of these.
MAT 6:30 30 But if God doth so clothe the grass of the field, which to-day is, and to-morrow is cast into the oven, shall he not much more clothe you, O ye of little faith?
MAT 6:31 31 Be not therefore anxious, saying, What shall we eat? or, What shall we drink? or, Wherewithal shall we be clothed?
MAT 6:32 32 For after all these things do the Gentiles seek; for your heavenly Father knoweth that ye have need of all these things.
MAT 6:33 33 But seek ye first his kingdom, and his righteousness; and all these things shall be added unto you.
MAT 6:34 34 Be not therefore anxious for the morrow: for the morrow will be anxious for itself. Sufficient unto the day is the evil thereof.
MAT 7:1 1 Judge not, that ye be not judged.
MAT 7:2 2 For with what judgment ye judge, ye shall be judged: and with what measure ye mete, it shall be measured unto you.
MAT 7:3 3 And why beholdest thou the mote that is in thy brother’s eye, but considerest not the beam that is in thine own eye?
MAT 7:4 4 Or how wilt thou say to thy brother, Let me cast out the mote out of thine eye; and lo, the beam is in thine own eye?
MAT 7:5 5 Thou hypocrite, cast out first the beam out of thine own eye; and then shalt thou see clearly to cast out the mote out of thy brother’s eye.
MAT 7:6 6 Give not that which is holy unto the dogs, neither cast your pearls before the swine, lest haply they trample them under their feet, and turn and rend you.
MAT 7:7 7 Ask, and it shall be given you; seek, and ye shall find; knock, and it shall be opened unto you:
MAT 7:8 8 for every one that asketh receiveth; and he that seeketh findeth; and to him that knocketh it shall be opened.
MAT 7:9 9 Or what man is there of you, who, if his son shall ask him for a loaf, will give him a stone;
MAT 7:10 10 or if he shall ask for a fish, will give him a serpent?
MAT 7:11 11 If ye then, being evil, know how to give good gifts unto your children, how much more shall your Father who is in heaven give good things to them that ask him?
MAT 7:12 12 All things therefore whatsoever ye would that men should do unto you, even so do ye also unto them: for this is the law and the prophets.
MAT 7:13 13 Enter ye in by the narrow gate: for wide is the gate, and broad is the way, that leadeth to destruction, and many are they that enter in thereby.
MAT 7:14 14 For narrow is the gate, and straitened the way, that leadeth unto life, and few are they that find it.
MAT 7:15 15 Beware of false prophets, who come to you in sheep’s clothing, but inwardly are ravening wolves.
MAT 7:16 16 By their fruits ye shall know them. Do men gather grapes of thorns, or figs of thistles?
MAT 7:17 17 Even so every good tree bringeth forth good fruit; but the corrupt tree bringeth forth evil fruit.
MAT 7:18 18 A good tree cannot bring forth evil fruit, neither can a corrupt tree bring forth good fruit.
MAT 7:19 19 Every tree that bringeth not forth good fruit is hewn down, and cast into the fire.
MAT 7:20 20 Therefore by their fruits ye shall know them.
MAT 7:21 21 Not every one that saith unto me, Lord, Lord, shall enter into the kingdom of heaven; but he that doeth the will of my Father who is in heaven.
MAT 7:22 22 Many will say to me in that day, Lord, Lord, did we not prophesy by thy name, and by thy name cast out demons, and by thy name do many mighty works?
MAT 7:23 23 And then will I profess unto them, I never knew you: depart from me, ye that work iniquity.
MAT 7:24 24 Every one therefore that heareth these words of mine, and doeth them, shall be likened unto a wise man, who built his house upon the rock:
MAT 7:25 25 and the rain descended, and the floods came, and the winds blew, and beat upon that house; and it fell not: for it was founded upon the rock.
MAT 7:26 26 And every one that heareth these words of mine, and doeth them not, shall be likened unto a foolish man, who built his house upon the sand:
MAT 7:27 27 and the rain descended, and the floods came, and the winds blew, and smote upon that house; and it fell: and great was the fall thereof.
MAT 7:28 28 And it came to pass, when Jesus had finished these words, the multitudes were astonished at his teaching:
MAT 7:29 29 for he taught them as one having authority, and not as their scribes.
MAT 8:1 1 And when he was come down from the mountain, great multitudes followed him.
MAT 8:2 2 And behold, there came to him a leper and worshipped him, saying, Lord, if thou wilt, thou canst make me clean.
MAT 8:3 3 And he stretched forth his hand, and touched him, saying, I will; be thou made clean. And straightway his leprosy was cleansed.
MAT 8:4 4 And Jesus saith unto him, See thou tell no man; but go, show thyself to the priest, and offer the gift that Moses commanded, for a testimony unto them.
MAT 8:5 5 And when he was entered into Capernaum, there came unto him a centurion, beseeching him,
MAT 8:6 6 and saying, Lord, my servant lieth in the house sick of the palsy, grievously tormented.
MAT 8:7 7 And he saith unto him, I will come and heal him.
MAT 8:8 8 And the centurion answered and said, Lord, I am not worthy that thou shouldest come under my roof; but only say the word, and my servant shall be healed.
MAT 8:9 9 For I also am a man under authority, having under myself soldiers: and I say to this one, Go, and he goeth; and to another, Come, and he cometh; and to my servant, Do this, and he doeth it.
MAT 8:10 10 And when Jesus heard it, he marvelled, and said to them that followed, Verily I say unto you, I have not found so great faith, no, not in Israel.
MAT 8:11 11 And I say unto you, that many shall come from the east and the west, and shall sit down with Abraham, and Isaac, and Jacob, in the kingdom of heaven:
MAT 8:12 12 but the sons of the kingdom shall be cast forth into the outer darkness: there shall be the weeping and the gnashing of teeth.
MAT 8:13 13 And Jesus said unto the centurion, Go thy way; as thou hast believed, so be it done unto thee. And the servant was healed in that hour.
MAT 8:14 14 And when Jesus was come into Peter’s house, he saw his wife’s mother lying sick of a fever.
MAT 8:15 15 And he touched her hand, and the fever left her; and she arose, and ministered unto him.
MAT 8:16 16 And when even was come, they brought unto him many possessed with demons: and he cast out the spirits with a word, and healed all that were sick:
MAT 8:17 17 that it might be fulfilled which was spoken through Isaiah the prophet, saying, Himself took our infirmities, and bare our diseases.
MAT 8:18 18 Now when Jesus saw great multitudes about him, he gave commandment to depart unto the other side.
MAT 8:19 19 And there came a scribe, and said unto him, Teacher, I will follow thee whithersoever thou goest.
MAT 8:20 20 And Jesus saith unto him, The foxes have holes, and the birds of the heaven have nests; but the Son of man hath not where to lay his head.
MAT 8:21 21 And another of the disciples said unto him, Lord, suffer me first to go and bury my father.
MAT 8:22 22 But Jesus saith unto him, Follow me; and leave the dead to bury their own dead.
MAT 8:23 23 And when he was entered into a boat, his disciples followed him.
MAT 8:24 24 And behold, there arose a great tempest in the sea, insomuch that the boat was covered with the waves: but he was asleep.
MAT 8:25 25 And they came to him, and awoke him, saying, Save, Lord; we perish.
MAT 8:26 26 And he saith unto them, Why are ye fearful, O ye of little faith? Then he arose, and rebuked the winds and the sea; and there was a great calm.
MAT 8:27 27 And the men marvelled, saying, What manner of man is this, that even the winds and the sea obey him?
MAT 8:28 28 And when he was come to the other side into the country of the Gadarenes, there met him two possessed with demons, coming forth out of the tombs, exceeding fierce, so that no man could pass by that way.
MAT 8:29 29 And behold, they cried out, saying, What have we to do with thee, thou Son of God? art thou come hither to torment us before the time?
MAT 8:30 30 Now there was afar off from them a herd of many swine feeding.
MAT 8:31 31 And the demons besought him, saying, If thou cast us out, send us away into the herd of swine.
MAT 8:32 32 And he said unto them, Go. And they came out, and went into the swine: and behold, the whole herd rushed down the steep into the sea, and perished in the waters.
MAT 8:33 33 And they that fed them fled, and went away into the city, and told everything, and what was befallen to them that were possessed with demons.
MAT 8:34 34 And behold, all the city came out to meet Jesus: and when they saw him, they besought him that he would depart from their borders.
MAT 9:1 1 And he entered into a boat, and crossed over, and came into his own city.
MAT 9:2 2 And behold, they brought to him a man sick of the palsy, lying on a bed: and Jesus seeing their faith said unto the sick of the palsy, Son, be of good cheer; thy sins are forgiven.
MAT 9:3 3 And behold, certain of the scribes said within themselves, This man blasphemeth.
MAT 9:4 4 And Jesus knowing their thoughts said, Wherefore think ye evil in your hearts?
MAT 9:5 5 For which is easier, to say, Thy sins are forgiven; or to say, Arise, and walk?
MAT 9:6 6 But that ye may know that the Son of man hath authority on earth to forgive sins (then saith he to the sick of the palsy), Arise, and take up thy bed, and go unto thy house.
MAT 9:7 7 And he arose, and departed to his house.
MAT 9:8 8 But when the multitudes saw it, they were afraid, and glorified God, who had given such authority unto men.
MAT 9:9 9 And as Jesus passed by from thence, he saw a man, called Matthew, sitting at the place of toll: and he saith unto him, Follow me. And he arose, and followed him.
MAT 9:10 10 And it came to pass, as he sat at meat in the house, behold, many publicans and sinners came and sat down with Jesus and his disciples.
MAT 9:11 11 And when the Pharisees saw it, they said unto his disciples, Why eateth your Teacher with the publicans and sinners?
MAT 9:12 12 But when he heard it, he said, They that are whole have no need of a physician, but they that are sick.
MAT 9:13 13 But go ye and learn what this meaneth, I desire mercy, and not sacrifice: for I came not to call the righteous, but sinners.
MAT 9:14 14 Then come to him the disciples of John, saying, Why do we and the Pharisees fast oft, but thy disciples fast not?
MAT 9:15 15 And Jesus said unto them, Can the sons of the bridechamber mourn, as long as the bridegroom is with them? but the days will come, when the bridegroom shall be taken away from them, and then will they fast.
MAT 9:16 16 And no man putteth a piece of undressed cloth upon an old garment; for that which should fill it up taketh from the garment, and a worse rent is made.
MAT 9:17 17 Neither do men put new wine into old wine-skins: else the skins burst, and the wine is spilled, and the skins perish: but they put new wine into fresh wine-skins, and both are preserved.
MAT 9:18 18 While he spake these things unto them, behold, there came a ruler, and worshipped him, saying, My daughter is even now dead: but come and lay thy hand upon her, and she shall live.
MAT 9:19 19 And Jesus arose, and followed him, and so did his disciples.
MAT 9:20 20 And behold, a woman, who had an issue of blood twelve years, came behind him, and touched the border of his garment:
MAT 9:21 21 for she said within herself, If I do but touch his garment, I shall be made whole.
MAT 9:22 22 But Jesus turning and seeing her said, Daughter, be of good cheer; thy faith hath made thee whole. And the woman was made whole from that hour.
MAT 9:23 23 And when Jesus came into the ruler’s house, and saw the flute-players, and the crowd making a tumult,
MAT 9:24 24 he said, Give place: for the damsel is not dead, but sleepeth. And they laughed him to scorn.
MAT 9:25 25 But when the crowd was put forth, he entered in, and took her by the hand; and the damsel arose.
MAT 9:26 26 And the fame hereof went forth into all that land.
MAT 9:27 27 And as Jesus passed by from thence, two blind men followed him, crying out, and saying, Have mercy on us, thou son of David.
MAT 9:28 28 And when he was come into the house, the blind men came to him: and Jesus saith unto them, Believe ye that I am able to do this? They say unto him, Yea, Lord.
MAT 9:29 29 Then touched he their eyes, saying, According to your faith be it done unto you.
MAT 9:30 30 And their eyes were opened. And Jesus strictly charged them, saying, See that no man know it.
MAT 9:31 31 But they went forth, and spread abroad his fame in all that land.
MAT 9:32 32 And as they went forth, behold, there was brought to him a dumb man possessed with a demon.
MAT 9:33 33 And when the demon was cast out, the dumb man spake: and the multitudes marvelled, saying, It was never so seen in Israel.
MAT 9:34 34 But the Pharisees said, By the prince of the demons casteth he out demons.
MAT 9:35 35 And Jesus went about all the cities and the villages, teaching in their synagogues, and preaching the gospel of the kingdom, and healing all manner of disease and all manner of sickness.
MAT 9:36 36 But when he saw the multitudes, he was moved with compassion for them, because they were distressed and scattered, as sheep not having a shepherd.
MAT 9:37 37 Then saith he unto his disciples, The harvest indeed is plenteous, but the laborers are few.
MAT 9:38 38 Pray ye therefore the Lord of the harvest, that he send forth laborers into his harvest.
MAT 10:1 1 And he called unto him his twelve disciples, and gave them authority over unclean spirits, to cast them out, and to heal all manner of disease and all manner of sickness.
MAT 10:2 2 Now the names of the twelve apostles are these: The first, Simon, who is called Peter, and Andrew his brother; James the son of Zebedee, and John his brother;
MAT 10:3 3 Philip, and Bartholomew; Thomas, and Matthew the publican; James the son of Alphæus, and Thaddæus;
MAT 10:4 4 Simon the Cananæan, and Judas Iscariot, who also betrayed him.
MAT 10:5 5 These twelve Jesus sent forth, and charged them, saying, Go not into any way of the Gentiles, and enter not into any city of the Samaritans:
MAT 10:6 6 but go rather to the lost sheep of the house of Israel.
MAT 10:7 7 And as ye go, preach, saying, The kingdom of heaven is at hand.
MAT 10:8 8 Heal the sick, raise the dead, cleanse the lepers, cast out demons: freely ye received, freely give.
MAT 10:9 9 Get you no gold, nor silver, nor brass in your purses;
MAT 10:10 10 no wallet for your journey, neither two coats, nor shoes, nor staff: for the laborer is worthy of his food.
MAT 10:11 11 And into whatsoever city or village ye shall enter, search out who in it is worthy; and there abide till ye go forth.
MAT 10:12 12 And as ye enter into the house, salute it.
MAT 10:13 13 And if the house be worthy, let your peace come upon it: but if it be not worthy, let your peace return to you.
MAT 10:14 14 And whosoever shall not receive you, nor hear your words, as ye go forth out of that house or that city, shake off the dust of your feet.
MAT 10:15 15 Verily I say unto you, It shall be more tolerable for the land of Sodom and Gomorrah in the day of judgment, than for that city.
MAT 10:16 16 Behold, I send you forth as sheep in the midst of wolves: be ye therefore wise as serpents, and harmless as doves.
MAT 10:17 17 But beware of men: for they will deliver you up to councils, and in their synagogues they will scourge you;
MAT 10:18 18 yea and before governors and kings shall ye be brought for my sake, for a testimony to them and to the Gentiles.
MAT 10:19 19 But when they deliver you up, be not anxious how or what ye shall speak: for it shall be given you in that hour what ye shall speak.
MAT 10:20 20 For it is not ye that speak, but the Spirit of your Father that speaketh in you.
MAT 10:21 21 And brother shall deliver up brother to death, and the father his child: and children shall rise up against parents, and cause them to be put to death.
MAT 10:22 22 And ye shall be hated of all men for my name’s sake: but he that endureth to the end, the same shall be saved.
MAT 10:23 23 But when they persecute you in this city, flee into the next: for verily I say unto you, Ye shall not have gone through the cities of Israel, till the Son of man be come.
MAT 10:24 24 A disciple is not above his teacher, nor a servant above his lord.
MAT 10:25 25 It is enough for the disciple that he be as his teacher, and the servant as his lord. If they have called the master of the house Beelzebub, how much more them of his household!
MAT 10:26 26 Fear them not therefore: for there is nothing covered, that shall not be revealed; and hid, that shall not be known.
MAT 10:27 27 What I tell you in the darkness, speak ye in the light; and what ye hear in the ear, proclaim upon the house-tops.
MAT 10:28 28 And be not afraid of them that kill the body, but are not able to kill the soul: but rather fear him who is able to destroy both soul and body in hell.
MAT 10:29 29 Are not two sparrows sold for a penny? and not one of them shall fall on the ground without your Father:
MAT 10:30 30 but the very hairs of your head are all numbered.
MAT 10:31 31 Fear not therefore: ye are of more value than many sparrows.
MAT 10:32 32 Every one therefore who shall confess me before men, him will I also confess before my Father who is in heaven.
MAT 10:33 33 But whosoever shall deny me before men, him will I also deny before my Father who is in heaven.
MAT 10:34 34 Think not that I came to send peace on the earth: I came not to send peace, but a sword.
MAT 10:35 35 For I came to set a man at variance against his father, and the daughter against her mother, and the daughter in law against her mother in law:
MAT 10:36 36 and a man’s foes shall be they of his own household.
MAT 10:37 37 He that loveth father or mother more than me is not worthy of me; and he that loveth son or daughter more than me is not worthy of me.
MAT 10:38 38 And he that doth not take his cross and follow after me, is not worthy of me.
MAT 10:39 39 He that findeth his life shall lose it; and he that loseth his life for my sake shall find it.
MAT 10:40 40 He that receiveth you receiveth me, and he that receiveth me receiveth him that sent me.
MAT 10:41 41 He that receiveth a prophet in the name of a prophet shall receive a prophet’s reward: and he that receiveth a righteous man in the name of a righteous man shall receive a righteous man’s reward.
MAT 10:42 42 And whosoever shall give to drink unto one of these little ones a cup of cold water only, in the name of a disciple, verily I say unto you he shall in no wise lose his reward.
MAT 11:1 1 And it came to pass when Jesus had finished commanding his twelve disciples, he departed thence to teach and preach in their cities.
MAT 11:2 2 Now when John heard in the prison the works of the Christ, he sent by his disciples
MAT 11:3 3 and said unto him, Art thou he that cometh, or look we for another?
MAT 11:4 4 And Jesus answered and said unto them, Go and tell John the things which ye hear and see:
MAT 11:5 5 the blind receive their sight, and the lame walk, the lepers are cleansed, and the deaf hear, and the dead are raised up, and the poor have good tidings preached to them.
MAT 11:6 6 And blessed is he, whosoever shall find no occasion of stumbling in me.
MAT 11:7 7 And as these went their way, Jesus began to say unto the multitudes concerning John, What went ye out into the wilderness to behold? a reed shaken with the wind?
MAT 11:8 8 But what went ye out to see? a man clothed in soft raiment ? Behold, they that wear soft raiment are in kings’ houses.
MAT 11:9 9 But wherefore went ye out? to see a prophet? Yea, I say unto you, and much more than a prophet.
MAT 11:10 10 This is he, of whom it is written,
MAT 11:11 Behold, I send my messenger before thy face,
MAT 11:12 Who shall prepare thy way before thee.
MAT 11:13 11 Verily I say unto you, Among them that are born of women there hath not arisen a greater than John the Baptist: yet he that is but little in the kingdom of heaven is greater than he.
MAT 11:14 12 And from the days of John the Baptist until now the kingdom of heaven suffereth violence, and men of violence take it by force.
MAT 11:15 13 For all the prophets and the law prophesied until John.
MAT 11:16 14 And if ye are willing to receive it , this is Elijah, that is to come.
MAT 11:17 15 He that hath ears to hear, let him hear.
MAT 11:18 16 But whereunto shall I liken this generation? It is like unto children sitting in the marketplaces, who call unto their fellows
MAT 11:19 17 and say, We piped unto you, and ye did not dance; we wailed, and ye did not mourn.
MAT 11:20 18 For John came neither eating nor drinking, and they say, He hath a demon.
MAT 11:21 19 The Son of man came eating and drinking, and they say, Behold, a gluttonous man and a winebibber, a friend of publicans and sinners! And wisdom is justified by her works.
MAT 11:22 20 Then began he to upbraid the cities wherein most of his mighty works were done, because they repented not.
MAT 11:23 21 Woe unto thee, Chorazin! woe unto thee, Bethsaida! for if the mighty works had been done in Tyre and Sidon which were done in you, they would have repented long ago in sackcloth and ashes.
MAT 11:24 22 But I say unto you, it shall be more tolerable for Tyre and Sidon in the day of judgment, than for you.
MAT 11:25 23 And thou, Capernaum, shalt thou be exalted unto heaven? thou shalt go down unto Hades: for if the mighty works had been done in Sodom which were done in thee, it would have remained until this day.
MAT 11:26 24 But I say unto you that it shall be more tolerable for the land of Sodom in the day of judgment, than for thee.
MAT 11:27 25 At that season Jesus answered and said, I thank thee, O Father, Lord of heaven and earth, that thou didst hide these things from the wise and understanding, and didst reveal them unto babes:
MAT 11:28 26 yea, Father, for so it was well-pleasing in thy sight.
MAT 11:29 27 All things have been delivered unto me of my Father: and no one knoweth the Son, save the Father; neither doth any know the Father, save the Son, and he to whomsoever the Son willeth to reveal him .
MAT 11:30 28 Come unto me, all ye that labor and are heavy laden, and I will give you rest.
MAT 11:31 29 Take my yoke upon you, and learn of me; for I am meek and lowly in heart: and ye shall find rest unto your souls.
MAT 11:32 30 For my yoke is easy, and my burden is light.
MAT 12:1 1 At that season Jesus went on the sabbath day through the grainfields; and his disciples were hungry and began to pluck ears and to eat.
MAT 12:2 2 But the Pharisees, when they saw it, said unto him, Behold, thy disciples do that which it is not lawful to do upon the sabbath.
MAT 12:3 3 But he said unto them, Have ye not read what David did, when he was hungry, and they that were with him;
MAT 12:4 4 how he entered into the house of God, and ate the showbread, which it was not lawful for him to eat, neither for them that were with him, but only for the priests?
MAT 12:5 5 Or have ye not read in the law, that on the sabbath day the priests in the temple profane the sabbath, and are guiltless?
MAT 12:6 6 But I say unto you, that one greater than the temple is here.
MAT 12:7 7 But if ye had known what this meaneth, I desire mercy, and not sacrifice, ye would not have condemned the guiltless.
MAT 12:8 8 For the Son of man is lord of the sabbath.
MAT 12:9 9 And he departed thence, and went into their synagogue:
MAT 12:10 10 and behold, a man having a withered hand. And they asked him, saying, Is it lawful to heal on the sabbath day? that they might accuse him.
MAT 12:11 11 And he said unto them, What man shall there be of you, that shall have one sheep, and if this fall into a pit on the sabbath day, will he not lay hold on it, and lift it out?
MAT 12:12 12 How much then is a man of more value than a sheep! Wherefore it is lawful to do good on the sabbath day.
MAT 12:13 13 Then saith he to the man, Stretch forth thy hand. And he stretched it forth; and it was restored whole, as the other.
MAT 12:14 14 But the Pharisees went out, and took counsel against him, how they might destroy him.
MAT 12:15 15 And Jesus perceiving it withdrew from thence: and many followed him; and he healed them all,
MAT 12:16 16 and charged them that they should not make him known:
MAT 12:17 17 that it might be fulfilled which was spoken through Isaiah the prophet, saying,
MAT 12:18 18 Behold, my servant whom I have chosen;
MAT 12:19 My beloved in whom my soul is well pleased:
MAT 12:20 I will put my Spirit upon him,
MAT 12:21 And he shall declare judgment to the Gentiles.
MAT 12:22 19 He shall not strive, nor cry aloud;
MAT 12:23 Neither shall any one hear his voice in the streets.
MAT 12:24 20 A bruised reed shall he not break,
MAT 12:25 And smoking flax shall he not quench,
MAT 12:26 Till he send forth judgment unto victory.
MAT 12:27 21 And in his name shall the Gentiles hope.
MAT 12:28 22 Then was brought unto him one possessed with a demon, blind and dumb: and he healed him, insomuch that the dumb man spake and saw.
MAT 12:29 23 And all the multitudes were amazed, and said, Can this be the son of David?
MAT 12:30 24 But when the Pharisees heard it, they said, This man doth not cast out demons, but by Beelzebub the prince of the demons.
MAT 12:31 25 And knowing their thoughts he said unto them, Every kingdom divided against itself is brought to desolation; and every city or house divided against itself shall not stand:
MAT 12:32 26 and if Satan casteth out Satan, he is divided against himself; how then shall his kingdom stand?
MAT 12:33 27 And if I by Beelzebub cast out demons, by whom do your sons cast them out? therefore shall they be your judges.
MAT 12:34 28 But if I by the Spirit of God cast out demons, then is the kingdom of God come upon you.
MAT 12:35 29 Or how can one enter into the house of the strong man , and spoil his goods, except he first bind the strong man ? and then he will spoil his house.
MAT 12:36 30 He that is not with me is against me; and he that gathereth not with me scattereth.
MAT 12:37 31 Therefore I say unto you, Every sin and blasphemy shall be forgiven unto men; but the blasphemy against the Spirit shall not be forgiven.
MAT 12:38 32 And whosoever shall speak a word against the Son of man, it shall be forgiven him; but whosoever shall speak against the Holy Spirit, it shall not be forgiven him, neither in this world, nor in that which is to come.
MAT 12:39 33 Either make the tree good, and its fruit good; or make the tree corrupt, and its fruit corrupt: for the tree is known by its fruit.
MAT 12:40 34 Ye offspring of vipers, how can ye, being evil, speak good things? for out of the abundance of the heart the mouth speaketh.
MAT 12:41 35 The good man out of his good treasure bringeth forth good things: and the evil man out of his evil treasure bringeth forth evil things.
MAT 12:42 36 And I say unto you, that every idle word that men shall speak, they shall give account thereof in the day of judgment.
MAT 12:43 37 For by thy words thou shalt be justified, and by thy words thou shalt be condemned.
MAT 12:44 38 Then certain of the scribes and Pharisees answered him, saying, Teacher, we would see a sign from thee.
MAT 12:45 39 But he answered and said unto them, An evil and adulterous generation seeketh after a sign; and there shall no sign be given to it but the sign of Jonah the prophet:
MAT 12:46 40 for as Jonah was three days and three nights in the belly of the whale; so shall the Son of man be three days and three nights in the heart of the earth.
MAT 12:47 41 The men of Nineveh shall stand up in the judgment with this generation, and shall condemn it: for they repented at the preaching of Jonah; and behold, a greater than Jonah is here.
MAT 12:48 42 The queen of the south shall rise up in the judgment with this generation, and shall condemn it: for she came from the ends of the earth to hear the wisdom of Solomon; and behold, a greater than Solomon is here.
MAT 12:49 43 But the unclean spirit, when he is gone out of the man, passeth through waterless places, seeking rest, and findeth it not.
MAT 12:50 44 Then he saith, I will return into my house whence I came out; and when he is come, he findeth it empty, swept, and garnished.
MAT 12:51 45 Then goeth he, and taketh with himself seven other spirits more evil than himself, and they enter in and dwell there: and the last state of that man becometh worse than the first. Even so shall it be also unto this evil generation.
MAT 12:52 46 While he was yet speaking to the multitudes, behold, his mother and his brethren stood without, seeking to speak to him.
MAT 12:53 47 And one said unto him, Behold, thy mother and thy brethren stand without, seeking to speak to thee.
MAT 12:54 48 But he answered and said unto him that told him, Who is my mother? and who are my brethren?
MAT 12:55 49 And he stretched forth his hand towards his disciples, and said, Behold, my mother and my brethren!
MAT 12:56 50 For whosoever shall do the will of my Father who is in heaven, he is my brother, and sister, and mother.
MAT 13:1 1 On that day went Jesus out of the house, and sat by the sea side.
MAT 13:2 2 And there were gathered unto him great multitudes, so that he entered into a boat, and sat; and all the multitude stood on the beach.
MAT 13:3 3 And he spake to them many things in parables, saying, Behold, the sower went forth to sow;
MAT 13:4 4 and as he sowed, some seeds fell by the way side, and the birds came and devoured them:
MAT 13:5 5 and others fell upon the rocky places, where they had not much earth: and straightway they sprang up, because they had no deepness of earth:
MAT 13:6 6 and when the sun was risen, they were scorched; and because they had no root, they withered away.
MAT 13:7 7 And others fell upon the thorns; and the thorns grew up and choked them:
MAT 13:8 8 and others fell upon the good ground, and yielded fruit, some a hundredfold, some sixty, some thirty.
MAT 13:9 9 He that hath ears, let him hear.
MAT 13:10 10 And the disciples came, and said unto him, Why speakest thou unto them in parables?
MAT 13:11 11 And he answered and said unto them, Unto you it is given to know the mysteries of the kingdom of heaven, but to them it is not given.
MAT 13:12 12 For whosoever hath, to him shall be given, and he shall have abundance: but whosoever hath not, from him shall be taken away even that which he hath.
MAT 13:13 13 Therefore speak I to them in parables; because seeing they see not, and hearing they hear not, neither do they understand.
MAT 13:14 14 And unto them is fulfilled the prophecy of Isaiah, which saith,
MAT 13:15 By hearing ye shall hear, and shall in no wise understand;
MAT 13:16 And seeing ye shall see, and shall in no wise perceive:
MAT 13:17 15 For this people’s heart is waxed gross,
MAT 13:18 And their ears are dull of hearing,
MAT 13:19 And their eyes they have closed;
MAT 13:20 Lest haply they should perceive with their eyes,
MAT 13:21 And hear with their ears,
MAT 13:22 And understand with their heart,
MAT 13:23 And should turn again,
MAT 13:24 And I should heal them.
MAT 13:25 16 But blessed are your eyes, for they see; and your ears, for they hear.
MAT 13:26 17 For verily I say unto you, that many prophets and righteous men desired to see the things which ye see, and saw them not; and to hear the things which ye hear, and heard them not.
MAT 13:27 18 Hear then ye the parable of the sower.
MAT 13:28 19 When any one heareth the word of the kingdom, and understandeth it not, then cometh the evil one , and snatcheth away that which hath been sown in his heart. This is he that was sown by the way side.
MAT 13:29 20 And he that was sown upon the rocky places, this is he that heareth the word, and straightway with joy receiveth it;
MAT 13:30 21 yet hath he not root in himself, but endureth for a while; and when tribulation or persecution ariseth because of the word, straightway he stumbleth.
MAT 13:31 22 And he that was sown among the thorns, this is he that heareth the word; and the care of the world, and the deceitfulness of riches, choke the word, and he becometh unfruitful.
MAT 13:32 23 And he that was sown upon the good ground, this is he that heareth the word, and understandeth it; who verily beareth fruit, and bringeth forth, some a hundredfold, some sixty, some thirty.
MAT 13:33 24 Another parable set he before them, saying, The kingdom of heaven is likened unto a man that sowed good seed in his field:
MAT 13:34 25 but while men slept, his enemy came and sowed tares also among the wheat, and went away.
MAT 13:35 26 But when the blade sprang up and brought forth fruit, then appeared the tares also.
MAT 13:36 27 And the servants of the householder came and said unto him, Sir, didst thou not sow good seed in thy field? whence then hath it tares?
MAT 13:37 28 And he said unto them, An enemy hath done this. And the servants say unto him, Wilt thou then that we go and gather them up?
MAT 13:38 29 But he saith, Nay; lest haply while ye gather up the tares, ye root up the wheat with them.
MAT 13:39 30 Let both grow together until the harvest: and in the time of the harvest I will say to the reapers, Gather up first the tares, and bind them in bundles to burn them; but gather the wheat into my barn.
MAT 13:40 31 Another parable set he before them, saying, The kingdom of heaven is like unto a grain of mustard seed, which a man took, and sowed in his field:
MAT 13:41 32 which indeed is less than all seeds; but when it is grown, it is greater than the herbs, and becometh a tree, so that the birds of the heaven come and lodge in the branches thereof.
MAT 13:42 33 Another parable spake he unto them; The kingdom of heaven is like unto leaven, which a woman took, and hid in three measures of meal, till it was all leavened.
MAT 13:43 34 All these things spake Jesus in parables unto the multitudes; and without a parable spake he nothing unto them:
MAT 13:44 35 that it might be fulfilled which was spoken through the prophet, saying,
MAT 13:45 I will open my mouth in parables;
MAT 13:46 I will utter things hidden from the foundation of the world.
MAT 13:47 36 Then he left the multitudes, and went into the house: and his disciples came unto him, saying, Explain unto us the parable of the tares of the field.
MAT 13:48 37 And he answered and said, He that soweth the good seed is the Son of man;
MAT 13:49 38 and the field is the world; and the good seed, these are the sons of the kingdom; and the tares are the sons of the evil one ;
MAT 13:50 39 and the enemy that sowed them is the devil: and the harvest is the end of the world; and the reapers are angels.
MAT 13:51 40 As therefore the tares are gathered up and burned with fire; so shall it be in the end of the world.
MAT 13:52 41 The Son of man shall send forth his angels, and they shall gather out of his kingdom all things that cause stumbling, and them that do iniquity,
MAT 13:53 42 and shall cast them into the furnace of fire: there shall be the weeping and the gnashing of teeth.
MAT 13:54 43 Then shall the righteous shine forth as the sun in the kingdom of their Father. He that hath ears, let him hear.
MAT 13:55 44 The kingdom of heaven is like unto a treasure hidden in the field; which a man found, and hid; and in his joy he goeth and selleth all that he hath, and buyeth that field.
MAT 13:56 45 Again, the kingdom of heaven is like unto a man that is a merchant seeking goodly pearls:
MAT 13:57 46 and having found one pearl of great price, he went and sold all that he had, and bought it.
MAT 13:58 47 Again, the kingdom of heaven is like unto a net, that was cast into the sea, and gathered of every kind:
MAT 13:59 48 which, when it was filled, they drew up on the beach; and they sat down, and gathered the good into vessels, but the bad they cast away.
MAT 13:60 49 So shall it be in the end of the world: the angels shall come forth, and sever the wicked from among the righteous,
MAT 13:61 50 and shall cast them into the furnace of fire: there shall be the weeping and the gnashing of teeth.
MAT 13:62 51 Have ye understood all these things? They say unto him, Yea.
MAT 13:63 52 And he said unto them, Therefore every scribe who hath been made a disciple to the kingdom of heaven is like unto a man that is a householder, who bringeth forth out of his treasure things new and old.
MAT 13:64 53 And it came to pass, when Jesus had finished these parables, he departed thence.
MAT 13:65 54 And coming into his own country he taught them in their synagogue, insomuch that they were astonished, and said, Whence hath this man this wisdom, and these mighty works?
MAT 13:66 55 Is not this the carpenter’s son? is not his mother called Mary? and his brethren, James, and Joseph, and Simon, and Judas?
MAT 13:67 56 And his sisters, are they not all with us? Whence then hath this man all these things?
MAT 13:68 57 And they were offended in him. But Jesus said unto them, A prophet is not without honor, save in his own country, and in his own house.
MAT 13:69 58 And he did not many mighty works there because of their unbelief.
MAT 14:1 1 At that season Herod the tetrarch heard the report concerning Jesus,
MAT 14:2 2 and said unto his servants, This is John the Baptist; he is risen from the dead; and therefore do these powers work in him.
MAT 14:3 3 For Herod had laid hold on John, and bound him, and put him in prison for the sake of Herodias, his brother Philip’s wife.
MAT 14:4 4 For John said unto him, It is not lawful for thee to have her.
MAT 14:5 5 And when he would have put him to death, he feared the multitude, because they counted him as a prophet.
MAT 14:6 6 But when Herod’s birthday came, the daughter of Herodias danced in the midst, and pleased Herod.
MAT 14:7 7 Whereupon he promised with an oath to give her whatsoever she should ask.
MAT 14:8 8 And she, being put forward by her mother, saith, Give me here on a platter the head of John the Baptist.
MAT 14:9 9 And the king was grieved; but for the sake of his oaths, and of them that sat at meat with him, he commanded it to be given;
MAT 14:10 10 and he sent and beheaded John in the prison.
MAT 14:11 11 And his head was brought on a platter, and given to the damsel: and she brought it to her mother.
MAT 14:12 12 And his disciples came, and took up the corpse, and buried him; and they went and told Jesus.
MAT 14:13 13 Now when Jesus heard it , he withdrew from thence in a boat, to a desert place apart: and when the multitudes heard thereof , they followed him on foot from the cities.
MAT 14:14 14 And he came forth, and saw a great multitude, and he had compassion on them, and healed their sick.
MAT 14:15 15 And when even was come, the disciples came to him, saying, The place is desert, and the time is already past; send the multitudes away, that they may go into the villages, and buy themselves food.
MAT 14:16 16 But Jesus said unto them, They have no need to go away; give ye them to eat.
MAT 14:17 17 And they say unto him, We have here but five loaves, and two fishes.
MAT 14:18 18 And he said, Bring them hither to me.
MAT 14:19 19 And he commanded the multitudes to sit down on the grass; and he took the five loaves, and the two fishes, and looking up to heaven, he blessed, and brake and gave the loaves to the disciples, and the disciples to the multitudes.
MAT 14:20 20 And they all ate, and were filled: and they took up that which remained over of the broken pieces, twelve baskets full.
MAT 14:21 21 And they that did eat were about five thousand men, besides women and children.
MAT 14:22 22 And straightway he constrained the disciples to enter into the boat, and to go before him unto the other side, till he should send the multitudes away.
MAT 14:23 23 And after he had sent the multitudes away, he went up into the mountain apart to pray: and when even was come, he was there alone.
MAT 14:24 24 But the boat was now in the midst of the sea, distressed by the waves; for the wind was contrary.
MAT 14:25 25 And in the fourth watch of the night he came unto them, walking upon the sea.
MAT 14:26 26 And when the disciples saw him walking on the sea, they were troubled, saying, It is a ghost; and they cried out for fear.
MAT 14:27 27 But straightway Jesus spake unto them, saying, Be of good cheer; it is I; be not afraid.
MAT 14:28 28 And Peter answered him and said, Lord, if it be thou, bid me come unto thee upon the waters.
MAT 14:29 29 And he said, Come. And Peter went down from the boat, and walked upon the waters to come to Jesus.
MAT 14:30 30 But when he saw the wind, he was afraid; and beginning to sink, he cried out, saying, Lord, save me.
MAT 14:31 31 And immediately Jesus stretched forth his hand, and took hold of him, and saith unto him, O thou of little faith, wherefore didst thou doubt?
MAT 14:32 32 And when they were gone up into the boat, the wind ceased.
MAT 14:33 33 And they that were in the boat worshipped him, saying, Of a truth thou art the Son of God.
MAT 14:34 34 And when they had crossed over, they came to the land, unto Gennesaret.
MAT 14:35 35 And when the men of that place knew him, they sent into all that region round about, and brought unto him all that were sick;
MAT 14:36 36 and they besought him that they might only touch the border of his garment: and as many as touched were made whole.
MAT 15:1 1 Then there come to Jesus from Jerusalem Pharisees and scribes, saying,
MAT 15:2 2 Why do thy disciples transgress the tradition of the elders? for they wash not their hands when they eat bread.
MAT 15:3 3 And he answered and said unto them, Why do ye also transgress the commandment of God because of your tradition?
MAT 15:4 4 For God said, Honor thy father and thy mother: and, He that speaketh evil of father or mother, let him die the death.
MAT 15:5 5 But ye say, Whosoever shall say to his father or his mother, That wherewith thou mightest have been profited by me is given to God ;
MAT 15:6 6 he shall not honor his father. And ye have made void the word of God because of your tradition.
MAT 15:7 7 Ye hypocrites, well did Isaiah prophesy of you, saying,
MAT 15:8 8 This people honoreth me with their lips;
MAT 15:9 But their heart is far from me.
MAT 15:10 9 But in vain do they worship me,
MAT 15:11 Teaching as their doctrines the precepts of men.
MAT 15:12 10 And he called to him the multitude, and said unto them, Hear, and understand:
MAT 15:13 11 Not that which entereth into the mouth defileth the man; but that which proceedeth out of the mouth, this defileth the man.
MAT 15:14 12 Then came the disciples, and said unto him, Knowest thou that the Pharisees were offended, when they heard this saying?
MAT 15:15 13 But he answered and said, Every plant which my heavenly Father planted not, shall be rooted up.
MAT 15:16 14 Let them alone: they are blind guides. And if the blind guide the blind, both shall fall into a pit.
MAT 15:17 15 And Peter answered and said unto him, Declare unto us the parable.
MAT 15:18 16 And he said, Are ye also even yet without understanding?
MAT 15:19 17 Perceive ye not, that whatsoever goeth into the mouth passeth into the belly, and is cast out into the draught?
MAT 15:20 18 But the things which proceed out of the mouth come forth out of the heart; and they defile the man.
MAT 15:21 19 For out of the heart come forth evil thoughts, murders, adulteries, fornications, thefts, false witness, railings:
MAT 15:22 20 these are the things which defile the man; but to eat with unwashen hands defileth not the man.
MAT 15:23 21 And Jesus went out thence, and withdrew into the parts of Tyre and Sidon.
MAT 15:24 22 And behold, a Canaanitish woman came out from those borders, and cried, saying, Have mercy on me, O Lord, thou son of David; my daughter is grievously vexed with a demon.
MAT 15:25 23 But he answered her not a word. And his disciples came and besought him, saying, Send her away; for she crieth after us.
MAT 15:26 24 But he answered and said, I was not sent but unto the lost sheep of the house of Israel.
MAT 15:27 25 But she came and worshipped him, saying, Lord, help me.
MAT 15:28 26 And he answered and said, It is not meet to take the children’s bread and cast it to the dogs.
MAT 15:29 27 But she said, Yea, Lord: for even the dogs eat of the crumbs which fall from their masters’ table.
MAT 15:30 28 Then Jesus answered and said unto her, O woman, great is thy faith: be it done unto thee even as thou wilt. And her daughter was healed from that hour.
MAT 15:31 29 And Jesus departed thence, and came nigh unto the sea of Galilee; and he went up into the mountain, and sat there.
MAT 15:32 30 And there came unto him great multitudes, having with them the lame, blind, dumb, maimed, and many others, and they cast them down at his feet; and he healed them:
MAT 15:33 31 insomuch that the multitude wondered, when they saw the dumb speaking, the maimed whole, and the lame walking, and the blind seeing: and they glorified the God of Israel.
MAT 15:34 32 And Jesus called unto him his disciples, and said, I have compassion on the multitude, because they continue with me now three days and have nothing to eat: and I would not send them away fasting, lest haply they faint on the way.
MAT 15:35 33 And the disciples say unto him, Whence should we have so many loaves in a desert place as to fill so great a multitude?
MAT 15:36 34 And Jesus said unto them, How many loaves have ye? And they said, Seven, and a few small fishes.
MAT 15:37 35 And he commanded the multitude to sit down on the ground;
MAT 15:38 36 and he took the seven loaves and the fishes; and he gave thanks and brake, and gave to the disciples, and the disciples to the multitudes.
MAT 15:39 37 And they all ate, and were filled: and they took up that which remained over of the broken pieces, seven baskets full.
MAT 15:40 38 And they that did eat were four thousand men, besides women and children.
MAT 15:41 39 And he sent away the multitudes, and entered into the boat, and came into the borders of Magadan.
MAT 16:1 1 And the Pharisees and Sadducees came, and trying him asked him to show them a sign from heaven.
MAT 16:2 2 But he answered and said unto them, When it is evening, ye say, It will be fair weather: for the heaven is red.
MAT 16:3 3 And in the morning, It will be foul weather to-day: for the heaven is red and lowering. Ye know how to discern the face of the heaven; but ye cannot discern the signs of the times.
MAT 16:4 4 An evil and adulterous generation seeketh after a sign; and there shall no sign be given unto it, but the sign of Jonah. And he left them, and departed.
MAT 16:5 5 And the disciples came to the other side and forgot to take bread.
MAT 16:6 6 And Jesus said unto them, Take heed and beware of the leaven of the Pharisees and Sadducees.
MAT 16:7 7 And they reasoned among themselves, saying, We took no bread.
MAT 16:8 8 And Jesus perceiving it said, O ye of little faith, why reason ye among yourselves, because ye have no bread?
MAT 16:9 9 Do ye not yet perceive, neither remember the five loaves of the five thousand, and how many baskets ye took up?
MAT 16:10 10 Neither the seven loaves of the four thousand, and how many baskets ye took up?
MAT 16:11 11 How is it that ye do not perceive that I spake not to you concerning bread? But beware of the leaven of the Pharisees and Sadducees.
MAT 16:12 12 Then understood they that he bade them not beware of the leaven of bread, but of the teaching of the Pharisees and Sadducees.
MAT 16:13 13 Now when Jesus came into the parts of Cæsarea Philippi, he asked his disciples, saying, Who do men say that the Son of man is?
MAT 16:14 14 And they said, Some say John the Baptist; some, Elijah; and others, Jeremiah, or one of the prophets.
MAT 16:15 15 He saith unto them, But who say ye that I am?
MAT 16:16 16 And Simon Peter answered and said, Thou art the Christ, the Son of the living God.
MAT 16:17 17 And Jesus answered and said unto him, Blessed art thou, Simon Bar-Jonah: for flesh and blood hath not revealed it unto thee, but my Father who is in heaven.
MAT 16:18 18 And I also say unto thee, that thou art Peter, and upon this rock I will build my church; and the gates of Hades shall not prevail against it.
MAT 16:19 19 I will give unto thee the keys of the kingdom of heaven: and whatsoever thou shalt bind on earth shall be bound in heaven; and whatsoever thou shalt loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven.
MAT 16:20 20 Then charged he the disciples that they should tell no man that he was the Christ.
MAT 16:21 21 From that time began Jesus to show unto his disciples, that he must go unto Jerusalem, and suffer many things of the elders and chief priests and scribes, and be killed, and the third day be raised up.
MAT 16:22 22 And Peter took him, and began to rebuke him, saying, Be it far from thee, Lord: this shall never be unto thee.
MAT 16:23 23 But he turned, and said unto Peter, Get thee behind me, Satan: thou art a stumbling-block unto me: for thou mindest not the things of God, but the things of men.
MAT 16:24 24 Then said Jesus unto his disciples, If any man would come after me, let him deny himself, and take up his cross, and follow me.
MAT 16:25 25 For whosoever would save his life shall lose it: and whosoever shall lose his life for my sake shall find it.
MAT 16:26 26 For what shall a man be profited, if he shall gain the whole world, and forfeit his life? or what shall a man give in exchange for his life?
MAT 16:27 27 For the Son of man shall come in the glory of his Father with his angels; and then shall he render unto every man according to his deeds.
MAT 16:28 28 Verily I say unto you, There are some of them that stand here, who shall in no wise taste of death, till they see the Son of man coming in his kingdom.
MAT 17:1 1 And after six days Jesus taketh with him Peter, and James, and John his brother, and bringeth them up into a high mountain apart:
MAT 17:2 2 and he was transfigured before them; and his face did shine as the sun, and his garments became white as the light.
MAT 17:3 3 And behold, there appeared unto them Moses and Elijah talking with him.
MAT 17:4 4 And Peter answered, and said unto Jesus, Lord, it is good for us to be here: if thou wilt, I will make here three tabernacles; one for thee, and one for Moses, and one for Elijah.
MAT 17:5 5 While he was yet speaking, behold, a bright cloud overshadowed them: and behold, a voice out of the cloud, saying, This is my beloved Son, in whom I am well pleased; hear ye him.
MAT 17:6 6 And when the disciples heard it, they fell on their face, and were sore afraid.
MAT 17:7 7 And Jesus came and touched them and said, Arise, and be not afraid.
MAT 17:8 8 And lifting up their eyes, they saw no one, save Jesus only.
MAT 17:9 9 And as they were coming down from the mountain, Jesus commanded them, saying, Tell the vision to no man, until the Son of man be risen from the dead.
MAT 17:10 10 And his disciples asked him, saying, Why then say the scribes that Elijah must first come?
MAT 17:11 11 And he answered and said, Elijah indeed cometh, and shall restore all things:
MAT 17:12 12 but I say unto you, that Elijah is come already, and they knew him not, but did unto him whatsoever they would. Even so shall the Son of man also suffer of them.
MAT 17:13 13 Then understood the disciples that he spake unto them of John the Baptist.
MAT 17:14 14 And when they were come to the multitude, there came to him a man, kneeling to him, and saying,
MAT 17:15 15 Lord, have mercy on my son: for he is epileptic, and suffereth grievously; for oft-times he falleth into the fire, and oft-times into the water.
MAT 17:16 16 And I brought him to thy disciples, and they could not cure him.
MAT 17:17 17 And Jesus answered and said, O faithless and perverse generation, how long shall I be with you? how long shall I bear with you? bring him hither to me.
MAT 17:18 18 And Jesus rebuked him; and the demon went out of him: and the boy was cured from that hour.
MAT 17:19 19 Then came the disciples to Jesus apart, and said, Why could not we cast it out?
MAT 17:20 20 And he saith unto them, Because of your little faith: for verily I say unto you, If ye have faith as a grain of mustard seed, ye shall say unto this mountain, Remove hence to yonder place; and it shall remove; and nothing shall be impossible unto you.
MAT 17:21 21
MAT 17:22 22 And while they abode in Galilee, Jesus said unto them, The Son of man shall be delivered up into the hands of men;
MAT 17:23 23 and they shall kill him, and the third day he shall be raised up. And they were exceeding sorry.
MAT 17:24 24 And when they were come to Capernaum, they that received the half-shekel came to Peter, and said, Doth not your teacher pay the half-shekel?
MAT 17:25 25 He saith, Yea. And when he came into the house, Jesus spake first to him, saying, What thinkest thou, Simon? the kings of the earth, from whom do they receive toll or tribute? from their sons, or from strangers?
MAT 17:26 26 And when he said, From strangers, Jesus said unto him, Therefore the sons are free.
MAT 17:27 27 But, lest we cause them to stumble, go thou to the sea, and cast a hook, and take up the fish that first cometh up; and when thou hast opened his mouth, thou shalt find a shekel: that take, and give unto them for me and thee.
MAT 18:1 1 In that hour came the disciples unto Jesus, saying, Who then is greatest in the kingdom of heaven?
MAT 18:2 2 And he called to him a little child, and set him in the midst of them,
MAT 18:3 3 and said, Verily I say unto you, Except ye turn, and become as little children, ye shall in no wise enter into the kingdom of heaven.
MAT 18:4 4 Whosoever therefore shall humble himself as this little child, the same is the greatest in the kingdom of heaven.
MAT 18:5 5 And whoso shall receive one such little child in my name receiveth me:
MAT 18:6 6 but whoso shall cause one of these little ones that believe on me to stumble, it is profitable for him that a great millstone should be hanged about his neck, and that he should be sunk in the depth of the sea.
MAT 18:7 7 Woe unto the world because of occasions of stumbling! for it must needs be that the occasions come; but woe to that man through whom the occasion cometh!
MAT 18:8 8 And if thy hand or thy foot causeth thee to stumble, cut it off, and cast it from thee: it is good for thee to enter into life maimed or halt, rather than having two hands or two feet to be cast into the eternal fire.
MAT 18:9 9 And if thine eye causeth thee to stumble, pluck it out, and cast it from thee: it is good for thee to enter into life with one eye, rather than having two eyes to be cast into the hell of fire.
MAT 18:10 10 See that ye despise not one of these little ones: for I say unto you, that in heaven their angels do always behold the face of my Father who is in heaven.
MAT 18:11 11
MAT 18:12 12 How think ye? if any man have a hundred sheep, and one of them be gone astray, doth he not leave the ninety and nine, and go unto the mountains, and seek that which goeth astray?
MAT 18:13 13 And if so be that he find it, verily I say unto you, he rejoiceth over it more than over the ninety and nine which have not gone astray.
MAT 18:14 14 Even so it is not the will of your Father who is in heaven, that one of these little ones should perish.
MAT 18:15 15 And if thy brother sin against thee, go, show him his fault between thee and him alone: if he hear thee, thou hast gained thy brother.
MAT 18:16 16 But if he hear thee not, take with thee one or two more, that at the mouth of two witnesses or three every word may be established.
MAT 18:17 17 And if he refuse to hear them, tell it unto the church: and if he refuse to hear the church also, let him be unto thee as the Gentile and the publican.
MAT 18:18 18 Verily I say unto you, What things soever ye shall bind on earth shall be bound in heaven; and what things soever ye shall loose on earth shall be loosed in heaven.
MAT 18:19 19 Again I say unto you, that if two of you shall agree on earth as touching anything that they shall ask, it shall be done for them of my Father who is in heaven.
MAT 18:20 20 For where two or three are gathered together in my name, there am I in the midst of them.
MAT 18:21 21 Then came Peter and said to him, Lord, how oft shall my brother sin against me, and I forgive him? until seven times?
MAT 18:22 22 Jesus saith unto him, I say not unto thee, Until seven times; but, Until seventy times seven.
MAT 18:23 23 Therefore is the kingdom of heaven likened unto a certain king, who would make a reckoning with his servants.
MAT 18:24 24 And when he had begun to reckon, one was brought unto him, that owed him ten thousand talents.
MAT 18:25 25 But forasmuch as he had not wherewith to pay, his lord commanded him to be sold, and his wife, and children, and all that he had, and payment to be made.
MAT 18:26 26 The servant therefore fell down and worshipped him, saying, Lord, have patience with me, and I will pay thee all.
MAT 18:27 27 And the lord of that servant, being moved with compassion, released him, and forgave him the debt.
MAT 18:28 28 But that servant went out, and found one of his fellow-servants, who owed him a hundred shillings: and he laid hold on him, and took him by the throat, saying, Pay what thou owest.
MAT 18:29 29 So his fellow-servant fell down and besought him, saying, Have patience with me, and I will pay thee.
MAT 18:30 30 And he would not: but went and cast him into prison, till he should pay that which was due.
MAT 18:31 31 So when his fellow-servants saw what was done, they were exceeding sorry, and came and told unto their lord all that was done.
MAT 18:32 32 Then his lord called him unto him, and saith to him, Thou wicked servant, I forgave thee all that debt, because thou besoughtest me:
MAT 18:33 33 shouldest not thou also have had mercy on thy fellow-servant, even as I had mercy on thee?
MAT 18:34 34 And his lord was wroth, and delivered him to the tormentors, till he should pay all that was due.
