MRK 5:41 41 And taking the child by the hand, he saith unto her, Talitha cumi; which is, being interpreted, Damsel, I say unto thee, Arise.
MRK 5:42 42 And straightway the damsel rose up, and walked; for she was twelve years old. And they were amazed straightway with a great amazement.
MRK 5:43 43 And he charged them much that no man should know this: and he commanded that something should be given her to eat.
MRK 6:1 1 And he went out from thence; and he cometh into his own country; and his disciples follow him.
MRK 6:2 2 And when the sabbath was come, he began to teach in the synagogue: and many hearing him were astonished, saying, Whence hath this man these things? and, What is the wisdom that is given unto this man, and what mean such mighty works wrought by his hands?
MRK 6:3 3 Is not this the carpenter, the son of Mary, and brother of James, and Joses, and Judas, and Simon? and are not his sisters here with us? And they were offended in him.
MRK 6:4 4 And Jesus said unto them, A prophet is not without honor, save in his own country, and among his own kin, and in his own house.
MRK 6:5 5 And he could there do no mighty work, save that he laid his hands upon a few sick folk, and healed them.
MRK 6:6 6 And he marvelled because of their unbelief.
MRK 6:7 And he went round about the villages teaching.
MRK 6:8 7 And he calleth unto him the twelve, and began to send them forth by two and two; and he gave them authority over the unclean spirits;
MRK 6:9 8 and he charged them that they should take nothing for their journey, save a staff only; no bread, no wallet, no money in their purse;
MRK 6:10 9 but to go shod with sandals: and, said he , put not on two coats.
MRK 6:11 10 And he said unto them, Wheresoever ye enter into a house, there abide till ye depart thence.
MRK 6:12 11 And whatsoever place shall not receive you, and they hear you not, as ye go forth thence, shake off the dust that is under your feet for a testimony unto them.
MRK 6:13 12 And they went out, and preached that men should repent.
MRK 6:14 13 And they cast out many demons, and anointed with oil many that were sick, and healed them.
MRK 6:15 14 And king Herod heard thereof ; for his name had become known: and he said, John the Baptizer is risen from the dead, and therefore do these powers work in him.
MRK 6:16 15 But others said, It is Elijah. And others said, It is a prophet, even as one of the prophets.
MRK 6:17 16 But Herod, when he heard thereof , said, John, whom I beheaded, he is risen.
MRK 6:18 17 For Herod himself had sent forth and laid hold upon John, and bound him in prison for the sake of Herodias, his brother Philip’s wife; for he had married her.
MRK 6:19 18 For John said unto Herod, It is not lawful for thee to have thy brother’s wife.
MRK 6:20 19 And Herodias set herself against him, and desired to kill him; and she could not;
MRK 6:21 20 for Herod feared John, knowing that he was a righteous and holy man, and kept him safe. And when he heard him, he was much perplexed; and he heard him gladly.
MRK 6:22 21 And when a convenient day was come, that Herod on his birthday made a supper to his lords, and the high captains, and the chief men of Galilee;
MRK 6:23 22 and when the daughter of Herodias herself came in and danced, she pleased Herod and them that sat at meat with him; and the king said unto the damsel, Ask of me whatsoever thou wilt, and I will give it thee.
MRK 6:24 23 And he sware unto her, Whatsoever thou shalt ask of me, I will give it thee, unto the half of my kingdom.
MRK 6:25 24 And she went out, and said unto her mother, What shall I ask? And she said, The head of John the Baptizer.
MRK 6:26 25 And she came in straightway with haste unto the king, and asked, saying, I will that thou forthwith give me on a platter the head of John the Baptist.
MRK 6:27 26 And the king was exceeding sorry; but for the sake of his oaths, and of them that sat at meat, he would not reject her.
MRK 6:28 27 And straightway the king sent forth a soldier of his guard, and commanded to bring his head: and he went and beheaded him in the prison,
MRK 6:29 28 and brought his head on a platter, and gave it to the damsel; and the damsel gave it to her mother.
MRK 6:30 29 And when his disciples heard thereof , they came and took up his corpse, and laid it in a tomb.
MRK 6:31 30 And the apostles gather themselves together unto Jesus; and they told him all things, whatsoever they had done, and whatsoever they had taught.
MRK 6:32 31 And he saith unto them, Come ye yourselves apart into a desert place, and rest a while. For there were many coming and going, and they had no leisure so much as to eat.
MRK 6:33 32 And they went away in the boat to a desert place apart.
MRK 6:34 33 And the people saw them going, and many knew them , and they ran together there on foot from all the cities, and outwent them.
MRK 6:35 34 And he came forth and saw a great multitude, and he had compassion on them, because they were as sheep not having a shepherd: and he began to teach them many things.
MRK 6:36 35 And when the day was now far spent, his disciples came unto him, and said, The place is desert, and the day is now far spent;
MRK 6:37 36 send them away, that they may go into the country and villages round about, and buy themselves somewhat to eat.
MRK 6:38 37 But he answered and said unto them, Give ye them to eat. And they say unto him, Shall we go and buy two hundred shillings’ worth of bread, and give them to eat?
MRK 6:39 38 And he saith unto them, How many loaves have ye? go and see. And when they knew, they say, Five, and two fishes.
MRK 6:40 39 And he commanded them that all should sit down by companies upon the green grass.
MRK 6:41 40 And they sat down in ranks, by hundreds, and by fifties.
MRK 6:42 41 And he took the five loaves and the two fishes, and looking up to heaven, he blessed, and brake the loaves; and he gave to the disciples to set before them; and the two fishes divided he among them all.
MRK 6:43 42 And they all ate, and were filled.
MRK 6:44 43 And they took up broken pieces, twelve basketfuls, and also of the fishes.
MRK 6:45 44 And they that ate the loaves were five thousand men.
MRK 6:46 45 And straightway he constrained his disciples to enter into the boat, and to go before him unto the other side to Bethsaida, while he himself sendeth the multitude away.
MRK 6:47 46 And after he had taken leave of them, he departed into the mountain to pray.
MRK 6:48 47 And when even was come, the boat was in the midst of the sea, and he alone on the land.
MRK 6:49 48 And seeing them distressed in rowing, for the wind was contrary unto them, about the fourth watch of the night he cometh unto them, walking on the sea; and he would have passed by them:
MRK 6:50 49 but they, when they saw him walking on the sea, supposed that it was a ghost, and cried out;
MRK 6:51 50 for they all saw him, and were troubled. But he straightway spake with them, and saith unto them, Be of good cheer: it is I; be not afraid.
MRK 6:52 51 And he went up unto them into the boat; and the wind ceased: and they were sore amazed in themselves;
MRK 6:53 52 for they understood not concerning the loaves, but their heart was hardened.
MRK 6:54 53 And when they had crossed over, they came to the land unto Gennesaret, and moored to the shore.
MRK 6:55 54 And when they were come out of the boat, straightway the people knew him,
MRK 6:56 55 and ran round about that whole region, and began to carry about on their beds those that were sick, where they heard he was.
MRK 6:57 56 And wheresoever he entered, into villages, or into cities, or into the country, they laid the sick in the marketplaces, and besought him that they might touch if it were but the border of his garment: and as many as touched him were made whole.
MRK 7:1 1 And there are gathered together unto him the Pharisees, and certain of the scribes, who had come from Jerusalem,
MRK 7:2 2 and had seen that some of his disciples ate their bread with defiled, that is, unwashen, hands.
MRK 7:3 3 (For the Pharisees, and all the Jews, except they wash their hands diligently, eat not, holding the tradition of the elders;
MRK 7:4 4 and when they come from the marketplace, except they bathe themselves, they eat not; and many other things there are, which they have received to hold, washings of cups, and pots, and brasen vessels.)
MRK 7:5 5 And the Pharisees and the scribes ask him, Why walk not thy disciples according to the tradition of the elders, but eat their bread with defiled hands?
MRK 7:6 6 And he said unto them, Well did Isaiah prophesy of you hypocrites, as it is written,
MRK 7:7 This people honoreth me with their lips,
MRK 7:8 But their heart is far from me.
MRK 7:9 7 But in vain do they worship me,
MRK 7:10 Teaching as their doctrines the precepts of men.
MRK 7:11 8 Ye leave the commandment of God, and hold fast the tradition of men.
MRK 7:12 9 And he said unto them, Full well do ye reject the commandment of God, that ye may keep your tradition.
MRK 7:13 10 For Moses said, Honor thy father and thy mother; and, He that speaketh evil of father or mother, let him die the death:
MRK 7:14 11 but ye say, If a man shall say to his father or his mother, That wherewith thou mightest have been profited by me is Corban, that is to say, Given to God ;
MRK 7:15 12 ye no longer suffer him to do aught for his father or his mother;
MRK 7:16 13 making void the word of God by your tradition, which ye have delivered: and many such like things ye do.
MRK 7:17 14 And he called to him the multitude again, and said unto them, Hear me all of you, and understand:
MRK 7:18 15 there is nothing from without the man, that going into him can defile him; but the things which proceed out of the man are those that defile the man.
MRK 7:19 16
MRK 7:20 17 And when he was entered into the house from the multitude, his disciples asked of him the parable.
MRK 7:21 18 And he saith unto them, Are ye so without understanding also? Perceive ye not, that whatsoever from without goeth into the man, it cannot defile him;
MRK 7:22 19 because it goeth not into his heart, but into his belly, and goeth out into the draught? This he said , making all meats clean.
MRK 7:23 20 And he said, That which proceedeth out of the man, that defileth the man.
MRK 7:24 21 For from within, out of the heart of men, evil thoughts proceed, fornications, thefts, murders, adulteries,
MRK 7:25 22 covetings, wickednesses, deceit, lasciviousness, an evil eye, railing, pride, foolishness:
MRK 7:26 23 all these evil things proceed from within, and defile the man.
MRK 7:27 24 And from thence he arose, and went away into the borders of Tyre and Sidon. And he entered into a house, and would have no man know it; and he could not be hid.
MRK 7:28 25 But straightway a woman, whose little daughter had an unclean spirit, having heard of him, came and fell down at his feet.
MRK 7:29 26 Now the woman was a Greek, a Syrophoenician by race. And she besought him that he would cast forth the demon out of her daughter.
MRK 7:30 27 And he said unto her, Let the children first be filled: for it is not meet to take the children’s bread and cast it to the dogs.
MRK 7:31 28 But she answered and saith unto him, Yea, Lord; even the dogs under the table eat of the children’s crumbs.
MRK 7:32 29 And he said unto her, For this saying go thy way; the demon is gone out of thy daughter.
MRK 7:33 30 And she went away unto her house, and found the child laid upon the bed, and the demon gone out.
MRK 7:34 31 And again he went out from the borders of Tyre, and came through Sidon unto the sea of Galilee, through the midst of the borders of Decapolis.
MRK 7:35 32 And they bring unto him one that was deaf, and had an impediment in his speech; and they beseech him to lay his hand upon him.
MRK 7:36 33 And he took him aside from the multitude privately, and put his fingers into his ears, and he spat, and touched his tongue;
MRK 7:37 34 and looking up to heaven, he sighed, and saith unto him, Ephphatha, that is, Be opened.
MRK 7:38 35 And his ears were opened, and the bond of his tongue was loosed, and he spake plain.
MRK 7:39 36 And he charged them that they should tell no man: but the more he charged them, so much the more a great deal they published it.
MRK 7:40 37 And they were beyond measure astonished, saying, He hath done all things well; he maketh even the deaf to hear, and the dumb to speak.
MRK 8:1 1 In those days, when there was again a great multitude, and they had nothing to eat, he called unto him his disciples, and saith unto them,
MRK 8:2 2 I have compassion on the multitude, because they continue with me now three days, and have nothing to eat:
MRK 8:3 3 and if I send them away fasting to their home, they will faint on the way; and some of them are come from far.
MRK 8:4 4 And his disciples answered him, Whence shall one be able to fill these men with bread here in a desert place?
MRK 8:5 5 And he asked them, How many loaves have ye? And they said, Seven.
MRK 8:6 6 And he commandeth the multitude to sit down on the ground: and he took the seven loaves, and having given thanks, he brake, and gave to his disciples, to set before them; and they set them before the multitude.
MRK 8:7 7 And they had a few small fishes: and having blessed them, he commanded to set these also before them.
MRK 8:8 8 And they ate, and were filled: and they took up, of broken pieces that remained over, seven baskets.
MRK 8:9 9 And they were about four thousand: and he sent them away.
MRK 8:10 10 And straightway he entered into the boat with his disciples, and came into the parts of Dalmanutha.
MRK 8:11 11 And the Pharisees came forth, and began to question with him, seeking of him a sign from heaven, trying him.
MRK 8:12 12 And he sighed deeply in his spirit, and saith, Why doth this generation seek a sign? verily I say unto you, There shall no sign be given unto this generation.
MRK 8:13 13 And he left them, and again entering into the boat departed to the other side.
MRK 8:14 14 And they forgot to take bread; and they had not in the boat with them more than one loaf.
MRK 8:15 15 And he charged them, saying, Take heed, beware of the leaven of the Pharisees and the leaven of Herod.
MRK 8:16 16 And they reasoned one with another, saying, We have no bread.
MRK 8:17 17 And Jesus perceiving it saith unto them, Why reason ye, because ye have no bread? do ye not yet perceive, neither understand? have ye your heart hardened?
MRK 8:18 18 Having eyes, see ye not? and having ears, hear ye not? and do ye not remember?
MRK 8:19 19 When I brake the five loaves among the five thousand, how many baskets full of broken pieces took ye up? They say unto him, Twelve.
MRK 8:20 20 And when the seven among the four thousand, how many basketfuls of broken pieces took ye up? And they say unto him, Seven.
MRK 8:21 21 And he said unto them, Do ye not yet understand?
MRK 8:22 22 And they come unto Bethsaida. And they bring to him a blind man, and beseech him to touch him.
MRK 8:23 23 And he took hold of the blind man by the hand, and brought him out of the village; and when he had spit on his eyes, and laid his hands upon him, he asked him, Seest thou aught?
MRK 8:24 24 And he looked up, and said, I see men; for I behold them as trees, walking.
MRK 8:25 25 Then again he laid his hands upon his eyes; and he looked stedfastly, and was restored, and saw all things clearly.
MRK 8:26 26 And he sent him away to his home, saying, Do not even enter into the village.
MRK 8:27 27 And Jesus went forth, and his disciples, into the villages of Cæsarea Philippi: and on the way he asked his disciples, saying unto them, Who do men say that I am?
MRK 8:28 28 And they told him, saying, John the Baptist; and others, Elijah; but others, One of the prophets.
MRK 8:29 29 And he asked them, But who say ye that I am? Peter answereth and saith unto him, Thou art the Christ.
MRK 8:30 30 And he charged them that they should tell no man of him.
MRK 8:31 31 And he began to teach them, that the Son of man must suffer many things, and be rejected by the elders, and the chief priests, and the scribes, and be killed, and after three days rise again.
MRK 8:32 32 And he spake the saying openly. And Peter took him, and began to rebuke him.
MRK 8:33 33 But he turning about, and seeing his disciples, rebuked Peter, and saith, Get thee behind me, Satan; for thou mindest not the things of God, but the things of men.
MRK 8:34 34 And he called unto him the multitude with his disciples, and said unto them, If any man would come after me, let him deny himself, and take up his cross, and follow me.
MRK 8:35 35 For whosoever would save his life shall lose it; and whosoever shall lose his life for my sake and the gospel’s shall save it.
MRK 8:36 36 For what doth it profit a man, to gain the whole world, and forfeit his life?
MRK 8:37 37 For what should a man give in exchange for his life?
MRK 8:38 38 For whosoever shall be ashamed of me and of my words in this adulterous and sinful generation, the Son of man also shall be ashamed of him, when he cometh in the glory of his Father with the holy angels.
MRK 9:1 1 And he said unto them, Verily I say unto you, There are some here of them that stand by , who shall in no wise taste of death, till they see the kingdom of God come with power.
MRK 9:2 2 And after six days Jesus taketh with him Peter, and James, and John, and bringeth them up into a high mountain apart by themselves: and he was transfigured before them;
MRK 9:3 3 and his garments became glistering, exceeding white, so as no fuller on earth can whiten them.
MRK 9:4 4 And there appeared unto them Elijah with Moses: and they were talking with Jesus.
MRK 9:5 5 And Peter answereth and saith to Jesus, Rabbi, it is good for us to be here: and let us make three tabernacles; one for thee, and one for Moses, and one for Elijah.
MRK 9:6 6 For he knew not what to answer; for they became sore afraid.
MRK 9:7 7 And there came a cloud overshadowing them: and there came a voice out of the cloud, This is my beloved Son: hear ye him.
MRK 9:8 8 And suddenly looking round about, they saw no one any more, save Jesus only with themselves.
MRK 9:9 9 And as they were coming down from the mountain, he charged them that they should tell no man what things they had seen, save when the Son of man should have risen again from the dead.
MRK 9:10 10 And they kept the saying, questioning among themselves what the rising again from the dead should mean.
MRK 9:11 11 And they asked him, saying, How is it that the scribes say that Elijah must first come?
MRK 9:12 12 And he said unto them, Elijah indeed cometh first, and restoreth all things: and how is it written of the Son of man, that he should suffer many things and be set at nought?
MRK 9:13 13 But I say unto you, that Elijah is come, and they have also done unto him whatsoever they would, even as it is written of him.
MRK 9:14 14 And when they came to the disciples, they saw a great multitude about them, and scribes questioning with them.
MRK 9:15 15 And straightway all the multitude, when they saw him, were greatly amazed, and running to him saluted him.
MRK 9:16 16 And he asked them, What question ye with them?
MRK 9:17 17 And one of the multitude answered him, Teacher, I brought unto thee my son, who hath a dumb spirit;
MRK 9:18 18 and wheresoever it taketh him, it dasheth him down: and he foameth, and grindeth his teeth, and pineth away: and I spake to thy disciples that they should cast it out; and they were not able.
MRK 9:19 19 And he answereth them and saith, O faithless generation, how long shall I be with you? how long shall I bear with you? bring him unto me.
MRK 9:20 20 And they brought him unto him: and when he saw him, straightway the spirit tare him grievously; and he fell on the ground, and wallowed foaming.
MRK 9:21 21 And he asked his father, How long time is it since this hath come unto him? And he said, From a child.
MRK 9:22 22 And oft-times it hath cast him both into the fire and into the waters, to destroy him: but if thou canst do anything, have compassion on us, and help us.
MRK 9:23 23 And Jesus said unto him, If thou canst! All things are possible to him that believeth.
MRK 9:24 24 Straightway the father of the child cried out, and said, I believe; help thou mine unbelief.
MRK 9:25 25 And when Jesus saw that a multitude came running together, he rebuked the unclean spirit, saying unto him, Thou dumb and deaf spirit, I command thee, come out of him, and enter no more into him.
MRK 9:26 26 And having cried out, and torn him much, he came out: and the boy became as one dead; insomuch that the more part said, He is dead.
MRK 9:27 27 But Jesus took him by the hand, and raised him up; and he arose.
MRK 9:28 28 And when he was come into the house, his disciples asked him privately, How is it that we could not cast it out?
MRK 9:29 29 And he said unto them, This kind can come out by nothing, save by prayer.
MRK 9:30 30 And they went forth from thence, and passed through Galilee; and he would not that any man should know it.
MRK 9:31 31 For he taught his disciples, and said unto them, The Son of man is delivered up into the hands of men, and they shall kill him; and when he is killed, after three days he shall rise again.
MRK 9:32 32 But they understood not the saying, and were afraid to ask him.
MRK 9:33 33 And they came to Capernaum: and when he was in the house he asked them, What were ye reasoning on the way?
MRK 9:34 34 But they held their peace: for they had disputed one with another on the way, who was the greatest.
MRK 9:35 35 And he sat down, and called the twelve; and he saith unto them, If any man would be first, he shall be last of all, and servant of all.
MRK 9:36 36 And he took a little child, and set him in the midst of them: and taking him in his arms, he said unto them,
MRK 9:37 37 Whosoever shall receive one of such little children in my name, receiveth me: and whosoever receiveth me, receiveth not me, but him that sent me.
MRK 9:38 38 John said unto him, Teacher, we saw one casting out demons in thy name; and we forbade him, because he followed not us.
MRK 9:39 39 But Jesus said, Forbid him not: for there is no man who shall do a mighty work in my name, and be able quickly to speak evil of me.
MRK 9:40 40 For he that is not against us is for us.
MRK 9:41 41 For whosoever shall give you a cup of water to drink, because ye are Christ’s, verily I say unto you, he shall in no wise lose his reward.
MRK 9:42 42 And whosoever shall cause one of these little ones that believe on me to stumble, it were better for him if a great millstone were hanged about his neck, and he were cast into the sea.
MRK 9:43 43 And if thy hand cause thee to stumble, cut it off: it is good for thee to enter into life maimed, rather than having thy two hands to go into hell, into the unquenchable fire.
MRK 9:44 44
MRK 9:45 45 And if thy foot cause thee to stumble, cut it off: it is good for thee to enter into life halt, rather than having thy two feet to be cast into hell.
MRK 9:46 46
MRK 9:47 47 And if thine eye cause thee to stumble, cast it out: it is good for thee to enter into the kingdom of God with one eye, rather than having two eyes to be cast into hell;
MRK 9:48 48 where their worm dieth not, and the fire is not quenched.
MRK 9:49 49 For every one shall be salted with fire.
MRK 9:50 50 Salt is good: but if the salt have lost its saltness, wherewith will ye season it? Have salt in yourselves, and be at peace one with another.
MRK 10:1 1 And he arose from thence, and cometh into the borders of Judæa and beyond the Jordan: and multitudes come together unto him again; and, as he was wont, he taught them again.
MRK 10:2 2 And there came unto him Pharisees, and asked him, Is it lawful for a man to put away his wife? trying him.
MRK 10:3 3 And he answered and said unto them, What did Moses command you?
MRK 10:4 4 And they said, Moses suffered to write a bill of divorcement, and to put her away.
MRK 10:5 5 But Jesus said unto them, For your hardness of heart he wrote you this commandment.
MRK 10:6 6 But from the beginning of the creation, Male and female made he them.
MRK 10:7 7 For this cause shall a man leave his father and mother, and shall cleave to his wife;
MRK 10:8 8 and the two shall become one flesh: so that they are no more two, but one flesh.
MRK 10:9 9 What therefore God hath joined together, let not man put asunder.
MRK 10:10 10 And in the house the disciples asked him again of this matter.
MRK 10:11 11 And he saith unto them, Whosoever shall put away his wife, and marry another, committeth adultery against her:
MRK 10:12 12 and if she herself shall put away her husband, and marry another, she committeth adultery.
MRK 10:13 13 And they were bringing unto him little children, that he should touch them: and the disciples rebuked them.
MRK 10:14 14 But when Jesus saw it, he was moved with indignation, and said unto them, Suffer the little children to come unto me; forbid them not: for to such belongeth the kingdom of God.
MRK 10:15 15 Verily I say unto you, Whosoever shall not receive the kingdom of God as a little child, he shall in no wise enter therein.
MRK 10:16 16 And he took them in his arms, and blessed them, laying his hands upon them.
MRK 10:17 17 And as he was going forth into the way, there ran one to him, and kneeled to him, and asked him, Good Teacher, what shall I do that I may inherit eternal life?
MRK 10:18 18 And Jesus said unto him, Why callest thou me good? none is good save one, even God.
MRK 10:19 19 Thou knowest the commandments, Do not kill, Do not commit adultery, Do not steal, Do not bear false witness, Do not defraud, Honor thy father and mother.
MRK 10:20 20 And he said unto him, Teacher, all these things have I observed from my youth.
MRK 10:21 21 And Jesus looking upon him loved him, and said unto him, One thing thou lackest: go, sell whatsoever thou hast, and give to the poor, and thou shalt have treasure in heaven: and come, follow me.
MRK 10:22 22 But his countenance fell at the saying, and he went away sorrowful: for he was one that had great possessions.
MRK 10:23 23 And Jesus looked round about, and saith unto his disciples, How hardly shall they that have riches enter into the kingdom of God!
MRK 10:24 24 And the disciples were amazed at his words. But Jesus answereth again, and saith unto them, Children, how hard is it for them that trust in riches to enter into the kingdom of God!
MRK 10:25 25 It is easier for a camel to go through a needle’s eye, than for a rich man to enter into the kingdom of God.
MRK 10:26 26 And they were astonished exceedingly, saying unto him, Then who can be saved?
MRK 10:27 27 Jesus looking upon them saith, With men it is impossible, but not with God: for all things are possible with God.
MRK 10:28 28 Peter began to say unto him, Lo, we have left all, and have followed thee.
MRK 10:29 29 Jesus said, Verily I say unto you, There is no man that hath left house, or brethren, or sisters, or mother, or father, or children, or lands, for my sake, and for the gospel’s sake,
MRK 10:30 30 but he shall receive a hundredfold now in this time, houses, and brethren, and sisters, and mothers, and children, and lands, with persecutions; and in the world to come eternal life.
MRK 10:31 31 But many that are first shall be last; and the last first.
MRK 10:32 32 And they were on the way, going up to Jerusalem; and Jesus was going before them: and they were amazed; and they that followed were afraid. And he took again the twelve, and began to tell them the things that were to happen unto him,
MRK 10:33 33 saying , Behold, we go up to Jerusalem; and the Son of man shall be delivered unto the chief priests and the scribes; and they shall condemn him to death, and shall deliver him unto the Gentiles:
MRK 10:34 34 and they shall mock him, and shall spit upon him, and shall scourge him, and shall kill him; and after three days he shall rise again.
MRK 10:35 35 And there come near unto him James and John, the sons of Zebedee, saying unto him, Teacher, we would that thou shouldest do for us whatsoever we shall ask of thee.
MRK 10:36 36 And he said unto them, What would ye that I should do for you?
MRK 10:37 37 And they said unto him, Grant unto us that we may sit, one on thy right hand, and one on thy left hand, in thy glory.
MRK 10:38 38 But Jesus said unto them, Ye know not what ye ask. Are ye able to drink the cup that I drink? or to be baptized with the baptism that I am baptized with?
MRK 10:39 39 And they said unto him, We are able. And Jesus said unto them, The cup that I drink ye shall drink; and with the baptism that I am baptized withal shall ye be baptized:
MRK 10:40 40 but to sit on my right hand or on my left hand is not mine to give; but it is for them for whom it hath been prepared.
MRK 10:41 41 And when the ten heard it, they began to be moved with indignation concerning James and John.
MRK 10:42 42 And Jesus called them to him, and saith unto them, Ye know that they who are accounted to rule over the Gentiles lord it over them; and their great ones exercise authority over them.
MRK 10:43 43 But it is not so among you: but whosoever would become great among you, shall be your minister;
MRK 10:44 44 and whosoever would be first among you, shall be servant of all.
MRK 10:45 45 For the Son of man also came not to be ministered unto, but to minister, and to give his life a ransom for many.
MRK 10:46 46 And they come to Jericho: and as he went out from Jericho, with his disciples and a great multitude, the son of Timæus, Bartimæus, a blind beggar, was sitting by the way side.
MRK 10:47 47 And when he heard that it was Jesus the Nazarene, he began to cry out, and say, Jesus, thou son of David, have mercy on me.
MRK 10:48 48 And many rebuked him, that he should hold his peace: but he cried out the more a great deal, Thou son of David, have mercy on me.
MRK 10:49 49 And Jesus stood still, and said, Call ye him. And they call the blind man, saying unto him, Be of good cheer: rise, he calleth thee.
MRK 10:50 50 And he, casting away his garment, sprang up, and came to Jesus.
MRK 10:51 51 And Jesus answered him, and said, What wilt thou that I should do unto thee? And the blind man said unto him, Rabboni, that I may receive my sight.
MRK 10:52 52 And Jesus said unto him, Go thy way; thy faith hath made thee whole. And straightway he received his sight, and followed him in the way.
MRK 11:1 1 And when they draw nigh unto Jerusalem, unto Bethphage and Bethany, at the mount of Olives, he sendeth two of his disciples,
MRK 11:2 2 and saith unto them, Go your way into the village that is over against you: and straightway as ye enter into it, ye shall find a colt tied, whereon no man ever yet sat; loose him, and bring him.
MRK 11:3 3 And if any one say unto you, Why do ye this? say ye, The Lord hath need of him; and straightway he will send him back hither.
MRK 11:4 4 And they went away, and found a colt tied at the door without in the open street; and they loose him.
MRK 11:5 5 And certain of them that stood there said unto them, What do ye, loosing the colt?
MRK 11:6 6 And they said unto them even as Jesus had said: and they let them go.
MRK 11:7 7 And they bring the colt unto Jesus, and cast on him their garments; and he sat upon him.
MRK 11:8 8 And many spread their garments upon the way; and others branches, which they had cut from the fields.
MRK 11:9 9 And they that went before, and they that followed, cried, Hosanna; Blessed is he that cometh in the name of the Lord:
MRK 11:10 10 Blessed is the kingdom that cometh, the kingdom of our father David: Hosanna in the highest.
MRK 11:11 11 And he entered into Jerusalem, into the temple; and when he had looked round about upon all things, it being now eventide, he went out unto Bethany with the twelve.
MRK 11:12 12 And on the morrow, when they were come out from Bethany, he hungered.
MRK 11:13 13 And seeing a fig tree afar off having leaves, he came, if haply he might find anything thereon: and when he came to it, he found nothing but leaves; for it was not the season of figs.
MRK 11:14 14 And he answered and said unto it, No man eat fruit from thee henceforward for ever. And his disciples heard it.
MRK 11:15 15 And they come to Jerusalem: and he entered into the temple, and began to cast out them that sold and them that bought in the temple, and overthrew the tables of the money-changers, and the seats of them that sold the doves;
MRK 11:16 16 and he would not suffer that any man should carry a vessel through the temple.
MRK 11:17 17 And he taught, and said unto them, Is it not written, My house shall be called a house of prayer for all the nations? but ye have made it a den of robbers.
MRK 11:18 18 And the chief priests and the scribes heard it, and sought how they might destroy him: for they feared him, for all the multitude was astonished at his teaching.
MRK 11:19 19 And every evening he went forth out of the city.
MRK 11:20 20 And as they passed by in the morning, they saw the fig tree withered away from the roots.
MRK 11:21 21 And Peter calling to remembrance saith unto him, Rabbi, behold, the fig tree which thou cursedst is withered away.
MRK 11:22 22 And Jesus answering saith unto them, Have faith in God.
MRK 11:23 23 Verily I say unto you, Whosoever shall say unto this mountain, Be thou taken up and cast into the sea; and shall not doubt in his heart, but shall believe that what he saith cometh to pass; he shall have it.
MRK 11:24 24 Therefore I say unto you, All things whatsoever ye pray and ask for, believe that ye receive them, and ye shall have them.
MRK 11:25 25 And whensoever ye stand praying, forgive, if ye have aught against any one; that your Father also who is in heaven may forgive you your trespasses.
MRK 11:26 26
MRK 11:27 27 And they come again to Jerusalem: and as he was walking in the temple, there come to him the chief priests, and the scribes, and the elders;
MRK 11:28 28 and they said unto him, By what authority doest thou these things? or who gave thee this authority to do these things?
MRK 11:29 29 And Jesus said unto them, I will ask of you one question, and answer me, and I will tell you by what authority I do these things.
MRK 11:30 30 The baptism of John, was it from heaven, or from men? answer me.
MRK 11:31 31 And they reasoned with themselves, saying, If we shall say, From heaven; he will say, Why then did ye not believe him?
MRK 11:32 32 But should we say, From men—they feared the people: for all verily held John to be a prophet.
MRK 11:33 33 And they answered Jesus and say, We know not. And Jesus saith unto them, Neither tell I you by what authority I do these things.
MRK 12:1 1 And he began to speak unto them in parables. A man planted a vineyard, and set a hedge about it, and digged a pit for the winepress, and built a tower, and let it out to husbandmen, and went into another country.
MRK 12:2 2 And at the season he sent to the husbandmen a servant, that he might receive from the husbandmen of the fruits of the vineyard.
MRK 12:3 3 And they took him, and beat him, and sent him away empty.
MRK 12:4 4 And again he sent unto them another servant; and him they wounded in the head, and handled shamefully.
MRK 12:5 5 And he sent another; and him they killed: and many others; beating some, and killing some.
MRK 12:6 6 He had yet one, a beloved son: he sent him last unto them, saying, They will reverence my son.
MRK 12:7 7 But those husbandmen said among themselves, This is the heir; come, let us kill him, and the inheritance shall be ours.
MRK 12:8 8 And they took him, and killed him, and cast him forth out of the vineyard.
MRK 12:9 9 What therefore will the lord of the vineyard do? he will come and destroy the husbandmen, and will give the vineyard unto others.
MRK 12:10 10 Have ye not read even this scripture:
MRK 12:11 The stone which the builders rejected,
MRK 12:12 The same was made the head of the corner;
MRK 12:13 11 This was from the Lord,
MRK 12:14 And it is marvellous in our eyes?
MRK 12:15 12 And they sought to lay hold on him; and they feared the multitude; for they perceived that he spake the parable against them: and they left him, and went away.
MRK 12:16 13 And they send unto him certain of the Pharisees and of the Herodians, that they might catch him in talk.
MRK 12:17 14 And when they were come, they say unto him, Teacher, we know that thou art true, and carest not for any one; for thou regardest not the person of men, but of a truth teachest the way of God: Is it lawful to give tribute unto Cæsar, or not?
MRK 12:18 15 Shall we give, or shall we not give? But he, knowing their hypocrisy, said unto them, Why make ye trial of me? bring me a denarius, that I may see it.
MRK 12:19 16 And they brought it. And he saith unto them, Whose is this image and superscription? And they said unto him, Cæsar’s.
MRK 12:20 17 And Jesus said unto them, Render unto Cæsar the things that are Cæsar’s, and unto God the things that are God’s. And they marvelled greatly at him.
MRK 12:21 18 And there come unto him Sadducees, who say that there is no resurrection; and they asked him, saying,
MRK 12:22 19 Teacher, Moses wrote unto us, If a man’s brother die, and leave a wife behind him, and leave no child, that his brother should take his wife, and raise up seed unto his brother.
MRK 12:23 20 There were seven brethren: and the first took a wife, and dying left no seed;
MRK 12:24 21 and the second took her, and died, leaving no seed behind him; and the third likewise:
MRK 12:25 22 and the seven left no seed. Last of all the woman also died.
MRK 12:26 23 In the resurrection whose wife shall she be of them? for the seven had her to wife.
MRK 12:27 24 Jesus said unto them, Is it not for this cause that ye err, that ye know not the scriptures, nor the power of God?
MRK 12:28 25 For when they shall rise from the dead, they neither marry, nor are given in marriage; but are as angels in heaven.
MRK 12:29 26 But as touching the dead, that they are raised; have ye not read in the book of Moses, in the place concerning the Bush, how God spake unto him, saying, I am the God of Abraham, and the God of Isaac, and the God of Jacob?
MRK 12:30 27 He is not the God of the dead, but of the living: ye do greatly err.
MRK 12:31 28 And one of the scribes came, and heard them questioning together, and knowing that he had answered them well, asked him, What commandment is the first of all?
MRK 12:32 29 Jesus answered, The first is, Hear, O Israel; The Lord our God, the Lord is one:
MRK 12:33 30 and thou shalt love the Lord thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy mind, and with all thy strength.
MRK 12:34 31 The second is this, Thou shalt love thy neighbor as thyself. There is none other commandment greater than these.
MRK 12:35 32 And the scribe said unto him, Of a truth, Teacher, thou hast well said that he is one; and there is none other but he:
MRK 12:36 33 and to love him with all the heart, and with all the understanding, and with all the strength, and to love his neighbor as himself, is much more than all whole burnt-offerings and sacrifices.
MRK 12:37 34 And when Jesus saw that he answered discreetly, he said unto him, Thou art not far from the kingdom of God. And no man after that durst ask him any question.
MRK 12:38 35 And Jesus answered and said, as he taught in the temple, How say the scribes that the Christ is the son of David?
MRK 12:39 36 David himself said in the Holy Spirit,
MRK 12:40 The Lord said unto my Lord,
MRK 12:41 Sit thou on my right hand,
MRK 12:42 Till I make thine enemies the footstool of thy feet.
MRK 12:43 37 David himself calleth him Lord; and whence is he his son? And the common people heard him gladly.
MRK 12:44 38 And in his teaching he said, Beware of the scribes, who desire to walk in long robes, and to have salutations in the marketplaces,
MRK 12:45 39 and chief seats in the synagogues, and chief places at feasts:
MRK 12:46 40 they that devour widows’ houses, and for a pretence make long prayers; these shall receive greater condemnation.
MRK 12:47 41 And he sat down over against the treasury, and beheld how the multitude cast money into the treasury: and many that were rich cast in much.
MRK 12:48 42 And there came a poor widow, and she cast in two mites, which make a farthing.
MRK 12:49 43 And he called unto him his disciples, and said unto them, Verily I say unto you, This poor widow cast in more than all they that are casting into the treasury:
MRK 12:50 44 for they all did cast in of their superfluity; but she of her want did cast in all that she had, even all her living.
MRK 13:1 1 And as he went forth out of the temple, one of his disciples saith unto him, Teacher, behold, what manner of stones and what manner of buildings!
MRK 13:2 2 And Jesus said unto him, Seest thou these great buildings? there shall not be left here one stone upon another, which shall not be thrown down.
MRK 13:3 3 And as he sat on the mount of Olives over against the temple, Peter and James and John and Andrew asked him privately,
MRK 13:4 4 Tell us, when shall these things be? and what shall be the sign when these things are all about to be accomplished?
MRK 13:5 5 And Jesus began to say unto them, Take heed that no man lead you astray.
MRK 13:6 6 Many shall come in my name, saying, I am he ; and shall lead many astray.
MRK 13:7 7 And when ye shall hear of wars and rumors of wars, be not troubled: these things must needs come to pass; but the end is not yet.
MRK 13:8 8 For nation shall rise against nation, and kingdom against kingdom; there shall be earthquakes in divers places; there shall be famines: these things are the beginning of travail.
MRK 13:9 9 But take ye heed to yourselves: for they shall deliver you up to councils; and in synagogues shall ye be beaten; and before governors and kings shall ye stand for my sake, for a testimony unto them.
MRK 13:10 10 And the gospel must first be preached unto all the nations.
MRK 13:11 11 And when they lead you to judgment , and deliver you up, be not anxious beforehand what ye shall speak: but whatsoever shall be given you in that hour, that speak ye; for it is not ye that speak, but the Holy Spirit.
MRK 13:12 12 And brother shall deliver up brother to death, and the father his child; and children shall rise up against parents, and cause them to be put to death.
MRK 13:13 13 And ye shall be hated of all men for my name’s sake: but he that endureth to the end, the same shall be saved.
MRK 13:14 14 But when ye see the abomination of desolation standing where he ought not (let him that readeth understand), then let them that are in Judæa flee unto the mountains:
MRK 13:15 15 and let him that is on the housetop not go down, nor enter in, to take anything out of his house:
MRK 13:16 16 and let him that is in the field not return back to take his cloak.
MRK 13:17 17 But woe unto them that are with child and to them that give suck in those days!
MRK 13:18 18 And pray ye that it be not in the winter.
MRK 13:19 19 For those days shall be tribulation, such as there hath not been the like from the beginning of the creation which God created until now, and never shall be.
MRK 13:20 20 And except the Lord had shortened the days, no flesh would have been saved; but for the elect’s sake, whom he chose, he shortened the days.
MRK 13:21 21 And then if any man shall say unto you, Lo, here is the Christ; or, Lo, there; believe it not:
MRK 13:22 22 for there shall arise false Christs and false prophets, and shall show signs and wonders, that they may lead astray, if possible, the elect.
MRK 13:23 23 But take ye heed: behold, I have told you all things beforehand.
MRK 13:24 24 But in those days, after that tribulation, the sun shall be darkened, and the moon shall not give her light,
MRK 13:25 25 and the stars shall be falling from heaven, and the powers that are in the heavens shall be shaken.
MRK 13:26 26 And then shall they see the Son of man coming in clouds with great power and glory.
MRK 13:27 27 And then shall he send forth the angels, and shall gather together his elect from the four winds, from the uttermost part of the earth to the uttermost part of heaven.
MRK 13:28 28 Now from the fig tree learn her parable: when her branch is now become tender, and putteth forth its leaves, ye know that the summer is nigh;
MRK 13:29 29 even so ye also, when ye see these things coming to pass, know ye that he is nigh, even at the doors.
MRK 13:30 30 Verily I say unto you, This generation shall not pass away, until all these things be accomplished.
MRK 13:31 31 Heaven and earth shall pass away: but my words shall not pass away.
MRK 13:32 32 But of that day or that hour knoweth no one, not even the angels in heaven, neither the Son, but the Father.
MRK 13:33 33 Take ye heed, watch and pray: for ye know not when the time is.
MRK 13:34 34 It is as when a man, sojourning in another country, having left his house, and given authority to his servants, to each one his work, commanded also the porter to watch.
MRK 13:35 35 Watch therefore: for ye know not when the lord of the house cometh, whether at even, or at midnight, or at cockcrowing, or in the morning;
MRK 13:36 36 lest coming suddenly he find you sleeping.
MRK 13:37 37 And what I say unto you I say unto all, Watch.
MRK 14:1 1 Now after two days was the feast of the passover and the unleavened bread: and the chief priests and the scribes sought how they might take him with subtlety, and kill him:
MRK 14:2 2 for they said, Not during the feast, lest haply there shall be a tumult of the people.
MRK 14:3 3 And while he was in Bethany in the house of Simon the leper, as he sat at meat, there came a woman having an alabaster cruse of ointment of pure nard very costly; and she brake the cruse, and poured it over his head.
MRK 14:4 4 But there were some that had indignation among themselves, saying , To what purpose hath this waste of the ointment been made?
MRK 14:5 5 For this ointment might have been sold for above three hundred shillings, and given to the poor. And they murmured against her.
MRK 14:6 6 But Jesus said, Let her alone; why trouble ye her? she hath wrought a good work on me.
MRK 14:7 7 For ye have the poor always with you, and whensoever ye will ye can do them good: but me ye have not always.
MRK 14:8 8 She hath done what she could; she hath anointed my body beforehand for the burying.
MRK 14:9 9 And verily I say unto you, Wheresoever the gospel shall be preached throughout the whole world, that also which this woman hath done shall be spoken of for a memorial of her.
MRK 14:10 10 And Judas Iscariot, he that was one of the twelve, went away unto the chief priests, that he might deliver him unto them.
MRK 14:11 11 And they, when they heard it, were glad, and promised to give him money. And he sought how he might conveniently deliver him unto them .
MRK 14:12 12 And on the first day of unleavened bread, when they sacrificed the passover, his disciples say unto him, Where wilt thou that we go and make ready that thou mayest eat the passover?
MRK 14:13 13 And he sendeth two of his disciples, and saith unto them, Go into the city, and there shall meet you a man bearing a pitcher of water: follow him;
MRK 14:14 14 and wheresoever he shall enter in, say to the master of the house, The Teacher saith, Where is my guest-chamber, where I shall eat the passover with my disciples?
MRK 14:15 15 And he will himself show you a large upper room furnished and ready: and there make ready for us.
MRK 14:16 16 And the disciples went forth, and came into the city, and found as he had said unto them: and they made ready the passover.
MRK 14:17 17 And when it was evening he cometh with the twelve.
MRK 14:18 18 And as they sat and were eating, Jesus said, Verily I say unto you, One of you shall betray me, even he that eateth with me.
MRK 14:19 19 They began to be sorrowful, and to say unto him one by one, Is it I?
MRK 14:20 20 And he said unto them, It is one of the twelve, he that dippeth with me in the dish.
MRK 14:21 21 For the Son of man goeth, even as it is written of him: but woe unto that man through whom the Son of man is betrayed! good were it for that man if he had not been born.
MRK 14:22 22 And as they were eating, he took bread, and when he had blessed, he brake it, and gave to them, and said, Take ye: this is my body.
MRK 14:23 23 And he took a cup, and when he had given thanks, he gave to them: and they all drank of it.
MRK 14:24 24 And he said unto them, This is my blood of the covenant, which is poured out for many.
MRK 14:25 25 Verily I say unto you, I shall no more drink of the fruit of the vine, until that day when I drink it new in the kingdom of God.
MRK 14:26 26 And when they had sung a hymn, they went out unto the mount of Olives.
MRK 14:27 27 And Jesus saith unto them, All ye shall be offended: for it is written, I will smite the shepherd, and the sheep shall be scattered abroad.
MRK 14:28 28 Howbeit, after I am raised up, I will go before you into Galilee.
MRK 14:29 29 But Peter said unto him, Although all shall be offended, yet will not I.
MRK 14:30 30 And Jesus saith unto him, Verily I say unto thee, that thou to-day, even this night, before the cock crow twice, shalt deny me thrice.
MRK 14:31 31 But he spake exceeding vehemently, If I must die with thee, I will not deny thee. And in like manner also said they all.
MRK 14:32 32 And they come unto a place which was named Gethsemane: and he saith unto his disciples, Sit ye here, while I pray.
MRK 14:33 33 And he taketh with him Peter and James and John, and began to be greatly amazed, and sore troubled.
MRK 14:34 34 And he saith unto them, My soul is exceeding sorrowful even unto death: abide ye here, and watch.
MRK 14:35 35 And he went forward a little, and fell on the ground, and prayed that, if it were possible, the hour might pass away from him.
MRK 14:36 36 And he said, Abba, Father, all things are possible unto thee; remove this cup from me: howbeit not what I will, but what thou wilt.
MRK 14:37 37 And he cometh, and findeth them sleeping, and saith unto Peter, Simon, sleepest thou? couldest thou not watch one hour?
MRK 14:38 38 Watch and pray, that ye enter not into temptation: the spirit indeed is willing, but the flesh is weak.
MRK 14:39 39 And again he went away, and prayed, saying the same words.
MRK 14:40 40 And again he came, and found them sleeping, for their eyes were very heavy; and they knew not what to answer him.
MRK 14:41 41 And he cometh the third time, and saith unto them, Sleep on now, and take your rest: it is enough; the hour is come; behold, the Son of man is betrayed into the hands of sinners.
MRK 14:42 42 Arise, let us be going: behold, he that betrayeth me is at hand.
MRK 14:43 43 And straightway, while he yet spake, cometh Judas, one of the twelve, and with him a multitude with swords and staves, from the chief priests and the scribes and the elders.
MRK 14:44 44 Now he that betrayed him had given them a token, saying, Whomsoever I shall kiss, that is he; take him, and lead him away safely.
MRK 14:45 45 And when he was come, straightway he came to him, and saith, Rabbi; and kissed him.
MRK 14:46 46 And they laid hands on him, and took him.
MRK 14:47 47 But a certain one of them that stood by drew his sword, and smote the servant of the high priest, and struck off his ear.
MRK 14:48 48 And Jesus answered and said unto them, Are ye come out, as against a robber, with swords and staves to seize me?
MRK 14:49 49 I was daily with you in the temple teaching, and ye took me not: but this is done that the scriptures might be fulfilled.
MRK 14:50 50 And they all left him, and fled.
MRK 14:51 51 And a certain young man followed with him, having a linen cloth cast about him, over his naked body : and they lay hold on him;
MRK 14:52 52 but he left the linen cloth, and fled naked.
MRK 14:53 53 And they led Jesus away to the high priest: and there come together with him all the chief priests and the elders and the scribes.
MRK 14:54 54 And Peter had followed him afar off, even within, into the court of the high priest; and he was sitting with the officers, and warming himself in the light of the fire .
MRK 14:55 55 Now the chief priests and the whole council sought witness against Jesus to put him to death; and found it not.
MRK 14:56 56 For many bare false witness against him, and their witness agreed not together.
MRK 14:57 57 And there stood up certain, and bare false witness against him, saying,
MRK 14:58 58 We heard him say, I will destroy this temple that is made with hands, and in three days I will build another made without hands.
MRK 14:59 59 And not even so did their witness agree together.
MRK 14:60 60 And the high priest stood up in the midst, and asked Jesus, saying, Answerest thou nothing? what is it which these witness against thee?
MRK 14:61 61 But he held his peace, and answered nothing. Again the high priest asked him, and saith unto him, Art thou the Christ, the Son of the Blessed?
MRK 14:62 62 And Jesus said, I am: and ye shall see the Son of man sitting at the right hand of Power, and coming with the clouds of heaven.
MRK 14:63 63 And the high priest rent his clothes, and saith, What further need have we of witnesses?
MRK 14:64 64 Ye have heard the blasphemy: what think ye? And they all condemned him to be worthy of death.
MRK 14:65 65 And some began to spit on him, and to cover his face, and to buffet him, and to say unto him, Prophesy: and the officers received him with blows of their hands.
MRK 14:66 66 And as Peter was beneath in the court, there cometh one of the maids of the high priest;
MRK 14:67 67 and seeing Peter warming himself, she looked upon him, and saith, Thou also wast with the Nazarene, even Jesus.
MRK 14:68 68 But he denied, saying, I neither know, nor understand what thou sayest: and he went out into the porch; and the cock crew.
MRK 14:69 69 And the maid saw him, and began again to say to them that stood by, This is one of them.
MRK 14:70 70 But he again denied it. And after a little while again they that stood by said to Peter, Of a truth thou art one of them; for thou art a Galilæan.
MRK 14:71 71 But he began to curse, and to swear, I know not this man of whom ye speak.
MRK 14:72 72 And straightway the second time the cock crew. And Peter called to mind the word, how that Jesus said unto him, Before the cock crow twice, thou shalt deny me thrice. And when he thought thereon, he wept.
MRK 15:1 1 And straightway in the morning the chief priests with the elders and scribes, and the whole council, held a consultation, and bound Jesus, and carried him away, and delivered him up to Pilate.
MRK 15:2 2 And Pilate asked him, Art thou the King of the Jews? And he answering saith unto him, Thou sayest.
MRK 15:3 3 And the chief priests accused him of many things.
MRK 15:4 4 And Pilate again asked him, saying, Answerest thou nothing? behold how many things they accuse thee of.
MRK 15:5 5 But Jesus no more answered anything; insomuch that Pilate marvelled.
MRK 15:6 6 Now at the feast he used to release unto them one prisoner, whom they asked of him.
MRK 15:7 7 And there was one called Barabbas, lying bound with them that had made insurrection, men who in the insurrection had committed murder.
MRK 15:8 8 And the multitude went up and began to ask him to do as he was wont to do unto them.
MRK 15:9 9 And Pilate answered them, saying, Will ye that I release unto you the King of the Jews?
MRK 15:10 10 For he perceived that for envy the chief priests had delivered him up.
MRK 15:11 11 But the chief priests stirred up the multitude, that he should rather release Barabbas unto them.
MRK 15:12 12 And Pilate again answered and said unto them, What then shall I do unto him whom ye call the King of the Jews?
MRK 15:13 13 And they cried out again, Crucify him.
MRK 15:14 14 And Pilate said unto them, Why, what evil hath he done? But they cried out exceedingly, Crucify him.
MRK 15:15 15 And Pilate, wishing to content the multitude, released unto them Barabbas, and delivered Jesus, when he had scourged him, to be crucified.
MRK 15:16 16 And the soldiers led him away within the court, which is the Prætorium; and they call together the whole band.
MRK 15:17 17 And they clothe him with purple, and platting a crown of thorns, they put it on him;
MRK 15:18 18 and they began to salute him, Hail, King of the Jews!
MRK 15:19 19 And they smote his head with a reed, and spat upon him, and bowing their knees worshipped him.
MRK 15:20 20 And when they had mocked him, they took off from him the purple, and put on him his garments. And they lead him out to crucify him.
MRK 15:21 21 And they compel one passing by, Simon of Cyrene, coming from the country, the father of Alexander and Rufus, to go with them , that he might bear his cross.
MRK 15:22 22 And they bring him unto the place Golgotha, which is, being interpreted, The place of a skull.
MRK 15:23 23 And they offered him wine mingled with myrrh: but he received it not.
MRK 15:24 24 And they crucify him, and part his garments among them, casting lots upon them, what each should take.
MRK 15:25 25 And it was the third hour, and they crucified him.
MRK 15:26 26 And the superscription of his accusation was written over, THE KING OF THE JEWS.
MRK 15:27 27 And with him they crucify two robbers; one on his right hand, and one on his left.
MRK 15:28 28
MRK 15:29 29 And they that passed by railed on him, wagging their heads, and saying, Ha! thou that destroyest the temple, and buildest it in three days,
MRK 15:30 30 save thyself, and come down from the cross.
MRK 15:31 31 In like manner also the chief priests mocking him among themselves with the scribes said, He saved others; himself he cannot save.
MRK 15:32 32 Let the Christ, the King of Israel, now come down from the cross, that we may see and believe. And they that were crucified with him reproached him.
MRK 15:33 33 And when the sixth hour was come, there was darkness over the whole land until the ninth hour.
MRK 15:34 34 And at the ninth hour Jesus cried with a loud voice, Eloi, Eloi, lama sabachthani? which is, being interpreted, My God, my God, why hast thou forsaken me?
MRK 15:35 35 And some of them that stood by, when they heard it, said, Behold, he calleth Elijah.
MRK 15:36 36 And one ran, and filling a sponge full of vinegar, put it on a reed, and gave him to drink, saying, Let be; let us see whether Elijah cometh to take him down.
MRK 15:37 37 And Jesus uttered a loud voice, and gave up the ghost.
MRK 15:38 38 And the veil of the temple was rent in two from the top to the bottom.
MRK 15:39 39 And when the centurion, who stood by over against him, saw that he so gave up the ghost, he said, Truly this man was the Son of God.
MRK 15:40 40 And there were also women beholding from afar: among whom were both Mary Magdalene, and Mary the mother of James the less and of Joses, and Salome;
MRK 15:41 41 who, when he was in Galilee, followed him, and ministered unto him; and many other women that came up with him unto Jerusalem.
MRK 15:42 42 And when even was now come, because it was the Preparation, that is, the day before the sabbath,
MRK 15:43 43 there came Joseph of Arimathæa, a councillor of honorable estate, who also himself was looking for the kingdom of God; and he boldly went in unto Pilate, and asked for the body of Jesus.
MRK 15:44 44 And Pilate marvelled if he were already dead: and calling unto him the centurion, he asked him whether he had been any while dead.
MRK 15:45 45 And when he learned it of the centurion, he granted the corpse to Joseph.
MRK 15:46 46 And he bought a linen cloth, and taking him down, wound him in the linen cloth, and laid him in a tomb which had been hewn out of a rock; and he rolled a stone against the door of the tomb.
MRK 15:47 47 And Mary Magdalene and Mary the mother of Joses beheld where he was laid.
MRK 16:1 1 And when the sabbath was past, Mary Magdalene, and Mary the mother of James, and Salome, bought spices, that they might come and anoint him.
MRK 16:2 2 And very early on the first day of the week, they come to the tomb when the sun was risen.
MRK 16:3 3 And they were saying among themselves, Who shall roll us away the stone from the door of the tomb?
MRK 16:4 4 and looking up, they see that the stone is rolled back: for it was exceeding great.
MRK 16:5 5 And entering into the tomb, they saw a young man sitting on the right side, arrayed in a white robe; and they were amazed.
MRK 16:6 6 And he saith unto them, Be not amazed: ye seek Jesus, the Nazarene, who hath been crucified: he is risen; he is not here: behold, the place where they laid him!
MRK 16:7 7 But go, tell his disciples and Peter, He goeth before you into Galilee: there shall ye see him, as he said unto you.
MRK 16:8 8 And they went out, and fled from the tomb; for trembling and astonishment had come upon them: and they said nothing to any one; for they were afraid.
MRK 16:9 9 Now when he was risen early on the first day of the week, he appeared first to Mary Magdalene, from whom he had cast out seven demons.
MRK 16:10 10 She went and told them that had been with him, as they mourned and wept.
MRK 16:11 11 And they, when they heard that he was alive, and had been seen of her, disbelieved.
MRK 16:12 12 And after these things he was manifested in another form unto two of them, as they walked, on their way into the country.
MRK 16:13 13 And they went away and told it unto the rest: neither believed they them.
MRK 16:14 14 And afterward he was manifested unto the eleven themselves as they sat at meat; and he upbraided them with their unbelief and hardness of heart, because they believed not them that had seen him after he was risen.
MRK 16:15 15 And he said unto them, Go ye into all the world, and preach the gospel to the whole creation.
MRK 16:16 16 He that believeth and is baptized shall be saved; but he that disbelieveth shall be condemned.
MRK 16:17 17 And these signs shall accompany them that believe: in my name shall they cast out demons; they shall speak with new tongues;
MRK 16:18 18 they shall take up serpents, and if they drink any deadly thing, it shall in no wise hurt them; they shall lay hands on the sick, and they shall recover.
MRK 16:19 19 So then the Lord Jesus, after he had spoken unto them, was received up into heaven, and sat down at the right hand of God.
MRK 16:20 20 And they went forth, and preached everywhere, the Lord working with them, and confirming the word by the signs that followed. Amen.
LUK 1:1 1 Forasmuch as many have taken in hand to draw up a narrative concerning those matters which have been fulfilled among us,
LUK 1:2 2 even as they delivered them unto us, who from the beginning were eyewitnesses and ministers of the word,
LUK 1:3 3 it seemed good to me also, having traced the course of all things accurately from the first, to write unto thee in order, most excellent Theophilus;
LUK 1:4 4 that thou mightest know the certainty concerning the things wherein thou wast instructed.
LUK 1:5 5 There was in the days of Herod, king of Judæa, a certain priest named Zacharias, of the course of Abijah: and he had a wife of the daughters of Aaron, and her name was Elisabeth.
LUK 1:6 6 And they were both righteous before God, walking in all the commandments and ordinances of the Lord blameless.
LUK 1:7 7 And they had no child, because that Elisabeth was barren, and they both were now well stricken in years.
LUK 1:8 8 Now it came to pass, while he executed the priest’s office before God in the order of his course,
LUK 1:9 9 according to the custom of the priest’s office, his lot was to enter into the temple of the Lord and burn incense.
LUK 1:10 10 And the whole multitude of the people were praying without at the hour of incense.
LUK 1:11 11 And there appeared unto him an angel of the Lord standing on the right side of the altar of incense.
LUK 1:12 12 And Zacharias was troubled when he saw him , and fear fell upon him.
LUK 1:13 13 But the angel said unto him, Fear not, Zacharias: because thy supplication is heard, and thy wife Elisabeth shall bear thee a son, and thou shalt call his name John.
LUK 1:14 14 And thou shalt have joy and gladness; and many shall rejoice at his birth.
LUK 1:15 15 For he shall be great in the sight of the Lord, and he shall drink no wine nor strong drink; and he shall be filled with the Holy Spirit, even from his mother’s womb.
LUK 1:16 16 And many of the children of Israel shall he turn unto the Lord their God.
LUK 1:17 17 And he shall go before his face in the spirit and power of Elijah, to turn the hearts of the fathers to the children, and the disobedient to walk in the wisdom of the just; to make ready for the Lord a people prepared for him .
LUK 1:18 18 And Zacharias said unto the angel, Whereby shall I know this? for I am an old man, and my wife well stricken in years.
LUK 1:19 19 And the angel answering said unto him, I am Gabriel, that stand in the presence of God; and I was sent to speak unto thee, and to bring thee these good tidings.
LUK 1:20 20 And behold, thou shalt be silent and not able to speak, until the day that these things shall come to pass, because thou believedst not my words, which shall be fulfilled in their season.
LUK 1:21 21 And the people were waiting for Zacharias, and they marvelled while he tarried in the temple.
LUK 1:22 22 And when he came out, he could not speak unto them: and they perceived that he had seen a vision in the temple: and he continued making signs unto them, and remained dumb.
LUK 1:23 23 And it came to pass, when the days of his ministration were fulfilled, he departed unto his house.
LUK 1:24 24 And after these days Elisabeth his wife conceived; and she hid herself five months, saying,
LUK 1:25 25 Thus hath the Lord done unto me in the days wherein he looked upon me , to take away my reproach among men.
LUK 1:26 26 Now in the sixth month the angel Gabriel was sent from God unto a city of Galilee, named Nazareth,
LUK 1:27 27 to a virgin betrothed to a man whose name was Joseph, of the house of David; and the virgin’s name was Mary.
LUK 1:28 28 And he came in unto her, and said, Hail, thou that art highly favored, the Lord is with thee.
LUK 1:29 29 But she was greatly troubled at the saying, and cast in her mind what manner of salutation this might be.
LUK 1:30 30 And the angel said unto her, Fear not, Mary: for thou hast found favor with God.
LUK 1:31 31 And behold, thou shalt conceive in thy womb, and bring forth a son, and shalt call his name JESUS.
LUK 1:32 32 He shall be great, and shall be called the Son of the Most High: and the Lord God shall give unto him the throne of his father David:
LUK 1:33 33 and he shall reign over the house of Jacob for ever; and of his kingdom there shall be no end.
LUK 1:34 34 And Mary said unto the angel, How shall this be, seeing I know not a man?
LUK 1:35 35 And the angel answered and said unto her, The Holy Spirit shall come upon thee, and the power of the Most High shall overshadow thee: wherefore also the holy thing which is begotten shall be called the Son of God.
LUK 1:36 36 And behold, Elisabeth thy kinswoman, she also hath conceived a son in her old age; and this is the sixth month with her that was called barren.
LUK 1:37 37 For no word from God shall be void of power.
LUK 1:38 38 And Mary said, Behold, the handmaid of the Lord; be it unto me according to thy word. And the angel departed from her.
LUK 1:39 39 And Mary arose in these days and went into the hill country with haste, into a city of Judah;
LUK 1:40 40 and entered into the house of Zacharias and saluted Elisabeth.
LUK 1:41 41 And it came to pass, when Elisabeth heard the salutation of Mary, the babe leaped in her womb; and Elisabeth was filled with the Holy Spirit;
LUK 1:42 42 and she lifted up her voice with a loud cry, and said, Blessed art thou among women, and blessed is the fruit of thy womb.
LUK 1:43 43 And whence is this to me, that the mother of my Lord should come unto me?
LUK 1:44 44 For behold, when the voice of thy salutation came into mine ears, the babe leaped in my womb for joy.
LUK 1:45 45 And blessed is she that believed; for there shall be a fulfilment of the things which have been spoken to her from the Lord.
LUK 1:46 46 And Mary said,
LUK 1:47 My soul doth magnify the Lord,
LUK 1:48 47 And my spirit hath rejoiced in God my Saviour.
LUK 1:49 48 For he hath looked upon the low estate of his handmaid:
LUK 1:50 For behold, from henceforth all generations shall call me blessed.
LUK 1:51 49 For he that is mighty hath done to me great things;
LUK 1:52 And holy is his name.
LUK 1:53 50 And his mercy is unto generations and generations
LUK 1:54 On them that fear him.
LUK 1:55 51 He hath showed strength with his arm;
LUK 1:56 He hath scattered the proud in the imagination of their heart.
LUK 1:57 52 He hath put down princes from their thrones,
LUK 1:58 And hath exalted them of low degree.
LUK 1:59 53 The hungry he hath filled with good things;
LUK 1:60 And the rich he hath sent empty away.
LUK 1:61 54 He hath given help to Israel his servant,
LUK 1:62 That he might remember mercy
LUK 1:63 55 (As he spake unto our fathers)
LUK 1:64 Toward Abraham and his seed for ever.
LUK 1:65 56 And Mary abode with her about three months, and returned unto her house.
LUK 1:66 57 Now Elisabeth’s time was fulfilled that she should be delivered; and she brought forth a son.
LUK 1:67 58 And her neighbors and her kinsfolk heard that the Lord had magnified his mercy towards her; and they rejoiced with her.
LUK 1:68 59 And it came to pass on the eighth day, that they came to circumcise the child; and they would have called him Zacharias, after the name of his father.
LUK 1:69 60 And his mother answered and said, Not so; but he shall be called John.
LUK 1:70 61 And they said unto her, There is none of thy kindred that is called by this name.
LUK 1:71 62 And they made signs to his father, what he would have him called.
LUK 1:72 63 And he asked for a writing tablet, and wrote, saying, His name is John. And they marvelled all.
LUK 1:73 64 And his mouth was opened immediately, and his tongue loosed , and he spake, blessing God.
LUK 1:74 65 And fear came on all that dwelt round about them: and all these sayings were noised abroad throughout all the hill country of Judæa.
LUK 1:75 66 And all that heard them laid them up in their heart, saying, What then shall this child be? For the hand of the Lord was with him.
LUK 1:76 67 And his father Zacharias was filled with the Holy Spirit, and prophesied, saying,
LUK 1:77 68 Blessed be the Lord, the God of Israel;
LUK 1:78 For he hath visited and wrought redemption for his people,
LUK 1:79 69 And hath raised up a horn of salvation for us
LUK 1:80 In the house of his servant David
LUK 1:81 70 (As he spake by the mouth of his holy prophets that have been from of old),
LUK 1:82 71 Salvation from our enemies, and from the hand of all that hate us;
LUK 1:83 72 To show mercy towards our fathers,
LUK 1:84 And to remember his holy covenant;
LUK 1:85 73 The oath which he sware unto Abraham our father,
LUK 1:86 74 To grant unto us that we being delivered out of the hand of our enemies
LUK 1:87 Should serve him without fear,
LUK 1:88 75 In holiness and righteousness before him all our days.
LUK 1:89 76 Yea and thou, child, shalt be called the prophet of the Most High:
LUK 1:90 For thou shalt go before the face of the Lord to make ready his ways;
LUK 1:91 77 To give knowledge of salvation unto his people
LUK 1:92 In the remission of their sins,
LUK 1:93 78 Because of the tender mercy of our God,
LUK 1:94 Whereby the dayspring from on high shall visit us,
LUK 1:95 79 To shine upon them that sit in darkness and the shadow of death;
LUK 1:96 To guide our feet into the way of peace.
LUK 1:97 80 And the child grew, and waxed strong in spirit, and was in the deserts till the day of his showing unto Israel.
LUK 2:1 1 Now it came to pass in those days, there went out a decree from Cæsar Augustus, that all the world should be enrolled.
LUK 2:2 2 This was the first enrolment made when Quirinius was governor of Syria.
LUK 2:3 3 And all went to enrol themselves, every one to his own city.
LUK 2:4 4 And Joseph also went up from Galilee, out of the city of Nazareth, into Judæa, to the city of David, which is called Bethlehem, because he was of the house and family of David;
LUK 2:5 5 to enrol himself with Mary, who was betrothed to him, being great with child.
LUK 2:6 6 And it came to pass, while they were there, the days were fulfilled that she should be delivered.
LUK 2:7 7 And she brought forth her firstborn son; and she wrapped him in swaddling clothes, and laid him in a manger, because there was no room for them in the inn.
LUK 2:8 8 And there were shepherds in the same country abiding in the field, and keeping watch by night over their flock.
LUK 2:9 9 And an angel of the Lord stood by them, and the glory of the Lord shone round about them: and they were sore afraid.
LUK 2:10 10 And the angel said unto them, Be not afraid; for behold, I bring you good tidings of great joy which shall be to all the people:
LUK 2:11 11 for there is born to you this day in the city of David a Saviour, who is Christ the Lord.
LUK 2:12 12 And this is the sign unto you: Ye shall find a babe wrapped in swaddling clothes, and lying in a manger.
LUK 2:13 13 And suddenly there was with the angel a multitude of the heavenly host praising God, and saying,
LUK 2:14 14 Glory to God in the highest,
LUK 2:15 And on earth peace among men in whom he is well pleased.
LUK 2:16 15 And it came to pass, when the angels went away from them into heaven, the shepherds said one to another, Let us now go even unto Bethlehem, and see this thing that is come to pass, which the Lord hath made known unto us.
LUK 2:17 16 And they came with haste, and found both Mary and Joseph, and the babe lying in the manger.
LUK 2:18 17 And when they saw it, they made known concerning the saying which was spoken to them about this child.
LUK 2:19 18 And all that heard it wondered at the things which were spoken unto them by the shepherds.
LUK 2:20 19 But Mary kept all these sayings, pondering them in her heart.
LUK 2:21 20 And the shepherds returned, glorifying and praising God for all the things that they had heard and seen, even as it was spoken unto them.
LUK 2:22 21 And when eight days were fulfilled for circumcising him, his name was called JESUS, which was so called by the angel before he was conceived in the womb.
LUK 2:23 22 And when the days of their purification according to the law of Moses were fulfilled, they brought him up to Jerusalem, to present him to the Lord
LUK 2:24 23 (as it is written in the law of the Lord, Every male that openeth the womb shall be called holy to the Lord),
LUK 2:25 24 and to offer a sacrifice according to that which is said in the law of the Lord, A pair of turtledoves, or two young pigeons.
LUK 2:26 25 And behold, there was a man in Jerusalem, whose name was Simeon; and this man was righteous and devout, looking for the consolation of Israel: and the Holy Spirit was upon him.
LUK 2:27 26 And it had been revealed unto him by the Holy Spirit, that he should not see death, before he had seen the Lord’s Christ.
LUK 2:28 27 And he came in the Spirit into the temple: and when the parents brought in the child Jesus, that they might do concerning him after the custom of the law,
LUK 2:29 28 then he received him into his arms, and blessed God, and said,
LUK 2:30 29 Now lettest thou thy servant depart, Lord,
LUK 2:31 According to thy word, in peace;
LUK 2:32 30 For mine eyes have seen thy salvation,
LUK 2:33 31 Which thou hast prepared before the face of all peoples;
LUK 2:34 32 A light for revelation to the Gentiles,
LUK 2:35 And the glory of thy people Israel.
LUK 2:36 33 And his father and his mother were marvelling at the things which were spoken concerning him;
LUK 2:37 34 and Simeon blessed them, and said unto Mary his mother, Behold, this child is set for the falling and the rising of many in Israel; and for a sign which is spoken against;
LUK 2:38 35 yea and a sword shall pierce through thine own soul; that thoughts out of many hearts may be revealed.
LUK 2:39 36 And there was one Anna, a prophetess, the daughter of Phanuel, of the tribe of Asher (she was of a great age, having lived with a husband seven years from her virginity,
LUK 2:40 37 and she had been a widow even unto fourscore and four years), who departed not from the temple, worshipping with fastings and supplications night and day.
LUK 2:41 38 And coming up at that very hour she gave thanks unto God, and spake of him to all them that were looking for the redemption of Jerusalem.
LUK 2:42 39 And when they had accomplished all things that were according to the law of the Lord, they returned into Galilee, to their own city Nazareth.
LUK 2:43 40 And the child grew, and waxed strong, filled with wisdom: and the grace of God was upon him.
LUK 2:44 41 And his parents went every year to Jerusalem at the feast of the passover.
LUK 2:45 42 And when he was twelve years old, they went up after the custom of the feast;
LUK 2:46 43 and when they had fulfilled the days, as they were returning, the boy Jesus tarried behind in Jerusalem; and his parents knew it not;
LUK 2:47 44 but supposing him to be in the company, they went a day’s journey; and they sought for him among their kinsfolk and acquaintance:
LUK 2:48 45 and when they found him not, they returned to Jerusalem, seeking for him.
LUK 2:49 46 And it came to pass, after three days they found him in the temple, sitting in the midst of the teachers, both hearing them, and asking them questions:
LUK 2:50 47 and all that heard him were amazed at his understanding and his answers.
LUK 2:51 48 And when they saw him, they were astonished; and his mother said unto him, Son, why hast thou thus dealt with us? behold, thy father and I sought thee sorrowing.
LUK 2:52 49 And he said unto them, How is it that ye sought me? knew ye not that I must be in my Father’s house?
LUK 2:53 50 And they understood not the saying which he spake unto them.
LUK 2:54 51 And he went down with them, and came to Nazareth; and he was subject unto them: and his mother kept all these sayings in her heart.
LUK 2:55 52 And Jesus advanced in wisdom and stature, and in favor with God and men.
LUK 3:1 1 Now in the fifteenth year of the reign of Tiberius Cæsar, Pontius Pilate being governor of Judæa, and Herod being tetrarch of Galilee, and his brother Philip tetrarch of the region of Ituræa and Trachonitis, and Lysanias tetrarch of Abilene,
LUK 3:2 2 in the high-priesthood of Annas and Caiaphas, the word of God came unto John the son of Zacharias in the wilderness.
LUK 3:3 3 And he came into all the region round about the Jordan, preaching the baptism of repentance unto remission of sins;
LUK 3:4 4 as it is written in the book of the words of Isaiah the prophet,
LUK 3:5 The voice of one crying in the wilderness,
LUK 3:6 Make ye ready the way of the Lord,
LUK 3:7 Make his paths straight.
LUK 3:8 5 Every valley shall be filled,
LUK 3:9 And every mountain and hill shall be brought low;
LUK 3:10 And the crooked shall become straight,
LUK 3:11 And the rough ways smooth;
LUK 3:12 6 And all flesh shall see the salvation of God.
LUK 3:13 7 He said therefore to the multitudes that went out to be baptized of him, Ye offspring of vipers, who warned you to flee from the wrath to come?
LUK 3:14 8 Bring forth therefore fruits worthy of repentance, and begin not to say within yourselves, We have Abraham to our father: for I say unto you, that God is able of these stones to raise up children unto Abraham.
LUK 3:15 9 And even now the axe also lieth at the root of the trees: every tree therefore that bringeth not forth good fruit is hewn down, and cast into the fire.
LUK 3:16 10 And the multitudes asked him, saying, What then must we do?
